## 9. قواعد سريعة لمن يبدأ الآن

```
✔ افحص آخر رقم هجرة قبل إنشاء واحدة جديدة
✔ أي منطق مالي جديد → دالة نقية + اختبار يستوردها من الإنتاج
✔ أي كتابة مخزون → applyStockDelta حصراً
✔ أي قيد محاسبي → insertPostedJournal حصراً
✔ أي بوابة رقابية → لا تعتمد على علَم يرسله العميل؛ استنتج من البيانات
✔ أي رمز بوابة خارجية → signPortalToken/verifyPortalToken حصراً
✔ أي استعلام/إدراج → tenant_id صريح؛ في الخدمات استعمل requireTenantScope لا resolveTenantContext
✔ كل INSERT على جدول فيه tenant_id يكتبه صراحةً — العمود NOT NULL DEFAULT '' لا يرمي، وtsc لا يحرسه (T1)
✔ قبل تضييق شرط WHERE بـtenant_id، افحص كل كاتب للجدول أولاً، وارمِّم الصفوف القائمة بهجرة (T3/F21)
✔ هل الجدول مُنطَّق أصلاً؟ الإجابة في ملفات الهجرة فقط، لا في database.types.ts (F20)
✔ شغّل أجنحة test/critical قبل وبعد أي تعديل في نواة مالية

✘ لا تُشغّل npm run build ولا git push تلقائياً (CLAUDE.md §2)
✘ لا تستخدم (db as any) على استعلام — يُخفي أخطاء المخطط
✘ لا تبنِ منطقاً على مطابقة نصوص عربية
✘ لا تكتب process.env.SECRET || '<ثابت>' لأي مفتاح يُثبت هوية — افشل بصوت عالٍ
✘ لا تثق بحمولة رمز بلا حالة: أعد التحقق من القاعدة (نشط؟ نفس المستأجر؟)
✘ لا تبنِ شرط استعلام شرطياً من حمولة خارجية (if (x) q = q.where(...)) — الغياب يحذف النطاق بدل أن يرفض (F19)
```
## P1-P3 hardening (30 September 2026)

- **Tenant identity:** in `CLOUD_SAAS`, only `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` is a platform tenant. The legacy `default` and `dev-tenant` aliases remain available only to self-contained/local modes.
- **Destructive demo operations:** every demo seed, wipe, and clear operation must complete an export and configured-folder backup before any delete begins. Backup errors abort the operation.
- **Demo accounts:** generated accounts receive random temporary passwords and `must_change_password=true`; known passwords are never displayed or stored.
- **Deployment schema safety:** the deployment takes an encrypted hourly database backup immediately before migrations. A migration or health failure therefore leaves a restorable pre-migration copy.
- **Shipping labels:** AWB requests are tenant-scoped. Production labels redirect only to an allow-listed Bosta URL; sandbox pages identify themselves as simulation and contain no inline script.

## POS, cashier shift, and returns hardening (2 October 2026)

- A cashier may have one open shift per tenant. Opening locks the cashier's user row, checks the branch and location in the same tenant, then inserts the shift and its number in one transaction. Migration `2040000000182` adds a partial unique index to enforce the rule across application instances. Existing duplicate open shifts must be resolved before that migration can succeed.
- POS sales requiring a shift lock an open shift for the cashier and sale branch. The sale, stock deduction, payments, drawer movement, and journal posting must commit together; a failed journal aborts the sale. Customer credit exposure is checked against a locked customer row, including partial payment balances.
- A sale below unit cost or a configured `products.min_selling_price`, after invoice discount allocation, requires a validated manager credential. The sale note records the approving identity and affected product IDs. The migration adds the optional minimum selling price column.
- A serialized sale line requires one unique, available tenant-owned IMEI/serial per base unit. Selling claims its rows in the sale transaction; cancelling or editing releases them; returning a serialized unit requires its original sold line and returns its serial to stock in the same transaction.
- An offline POS invoice must carry a stable `x-idempotency-key`. All retries use that key and the original payload; the browser queue is tenant/account scoped, and legacy queue entries without ownership must not be replayed automatically into a SaaS tenant.
- Store credit is a liability in account `2150`: sales debit it when redeemed and returns credit it when granted. A return without a linked customer cannot settle to store credit.
- Sales and purchase returns lock original invoice lines and check both cumulative line and product quantities, including earlier returns without line IDs. A cash sales refund requires the cashier's open shift on the original branch and updates its expected cash in the same transaction. Shift close stores the signed counted-minus-expected difference in the existing `variance` column.
- POS terminal sessions are tenant-bound. ECR card payments record authorization codes (AUTH), retrieval reference numbers (RRN), card scheme (Mada, KNET, Visa, Mastercard, Meeza), masked PAN (last 4 digits), and terminal TID on the sales table, sale_payments table, audit log, and printed thermal slips (customer and merchant copies). The terminal integration supports Geidea (Mada / Saudi), Network International (UAE), KNET (Kuwait), Fawry / Paymob (Egypt), and Sandbox simulator mode with auto-finalize and zero cashier error. Real LAN/Wi-Fi ECR payments are routed via outbound HTTPS Cloud Relay or local bridge, strictly prohibiting mock auto-approvals (AUTH-xxxxxx) on real devices, with bidirectional remote cancel support and exact currency minor/major unit compliance.

## Purchases, transfers, and stock counts (2 October 2026)

- Supplier bill references are unique per tenant and supplier while a purchase remains active. Migration `2040000000183` creates the unique index; existing active duplicates must be reconciled before deployment. Application-side duplicate checks remain advisory; the index closes the concurrent insert gap.
- Direct and scheduled supplier payments honor `x-idempotency-key` for replay-safe requests. The direct path locks the supplier balance before checking it; the scheduled path locks both the supplier and installment before recomputing the unpaid amount. Purchase and GRN moving-average costing reads the current cost while the product row is locked in the same transaction.
- In-transit transfer creation serializes per tenant; receipt and cancellation require an existing in-transit location. Receipts reject unknown, repeated, negative, non-finite, or excess quantities. Product rows are locked in ascending order before location stock rows, and the shared stock ledger prevents source stock from becoming negative.
- Migration `2040000000184` tracks posting progress per stock-count item. Sessions move `draft → posting → posted` in chunks of 64 products. Each chunk atomically commits stock changes, a balancing variance journal, and item progress; a retry resumes unposted items. If an unposted item is stale, an authorized operator must physically recount it through `POST /api/stock-count-sessions/:id/items/:itemId/recount` with a reason, then resume posting. Committed chunks are never automatically reversed.

## Shared database scale guardrails (2 October 2026)

- Delivery order/settlement lists, van-trip audit details, and maritime job journal lines have bounded pages (default 50, maximum 200). Delivery KPIs aggregate in PostgreSQL. KDS reads at most 200 oldest outstanding tickets within its 14-hour window, excludes served ticket IDs, and reports `truncated` if the queue overflows; runtime state older than 24 hours is pruned. Fraud radar computes exact event counts per cashier in SQL and examines at most 2,000 detailed audit rows for its monetary estimate; it reports `sampled: true` when that estimate is incomplete. The sampled monetary estimate must not be presented as a complete historical audit.
- Migration `2040000000185` adds tenant-prefixed indexes for representative chronology and settlement, job cost-center journal lines, and cashier audit chronology. Earlier migrations already provide tenant-prefixed sales, sale-item, stock-movement, journal-entry, and audit chronology indexes; do not duplicate them. Large index builds in the transactional migration runner can block writes; deploy during a maintenance window and observe PostgreSQL lock waits.
- The backend pool defaults to 25 connections and caps a configured pool at 40. A statement has at most 30 seconds, idle transactions are terminated after 60 seconds, and idle-client errors are handled without crashing Node. Cache entries are scoped by tenant or globally unique storefront slug; dashboard caches retain at most 256 views and the large storefront catalog cache retains at most 16 catalogs.
- `backend/test/critical/multi-tenant-1000-scale.spec.ts` has an in-memory concurrency contract in the standard critical suite and an opt-in staging probe with 1,000 bounded reads and advisory locks. Neither exercises real financial write transactions on PostgreSQL. The public storefront still returns a full catalog for legacy clients; its cache is bounded, but the response itself remains a scale risk until clients migrate to paged catalog requests.
- Aged-debt and AR dunning calculations still materialize multi-year unpaid invoice sets. They now reject scopes above 5,000 rows instead of silently returning partial financial figures or exhausting process memory. They must be moved to paged/windowed SQL aggregation before claiming full 1,000-tenant commercial readiness; applying a simple `LIMIT` and reporting a partial balance is prohibited.

### 2.6.1 Rocket-Speed sprint follow-up (2 October 2026)

The authenticated master endpoints use private conditional caching and weak ETags. Auth/session and settings fast paths are bounded, short-lived in-process LRUs keyed by session ID or tenant and permission scope; explicit invalidation on writes remains required. Aging and AR collections keep tenant filters inside SQL window aggregations, page partner output, and batch AR writes. The paged public storefront query now fetches only the requested products, stock and reviews, while the legacy no-parameter storefront contract still returns the full catalog until frontend search, facets, and sorting move server-side (O76). Sidebar prefetch targets hovered routes during idle time and catalog table derivations are memoized. Deployment benchmarks, Brotli negotiation, typechecks and tests remain with the local pair under the owner's no-test instruction; no <100 ms latency claim is established by this code change alone.

## Phase 6: E-invoice and manual-stock concurrency (3 October 2026)

- ZATCA signing locks the tenant-owned sale before the branch EGS unit. The unit's `last_icv` and `last_invoice_hash` advance in the same transaction that saves the sale's UUID, ICV, PIH, XML, hash, QR and EGS ID. Never allocate another ICV for a sale already signed; retransmission must reuse its persisted package. The first unit for a branch is created only after locking the tenant row and rechecking. Migration `2040000000187` rejects duplicate `(tenant_id, zatca_egs_id, zatca_icv)` values. Historical duplicates must be reconciled before this unique index can deploy.
- Manual stock writes acquire `products FOR UPDATE` before `product_location_stock`, read cost under that lock, and record the signed delta returned by the shared stock ledger. A zero-cost, negative-stock, non-finite or zero-value adjustment fails the transaction. Its journal must be posted before the transaction commits. The same rule applies to damaged-stock write-offs and stock-count variance chunks.
- The default `multi-tenant-1000-scale.spec.ts` mode is an in-memory lock-order and integer-cent accounting contract over ten tenants; `SCALE_TEST_ALLOW_DB=1` is the separate staging read and advisory-lock probe. Neither mode currently executes concurrent production sales and transfers against PostgreSQL. Real write-path load, TypeScript, migration dry-run and CI are still required before claiming measured 1,000-tenant readiness.

## Storefront payment and offer follow-up (3 October 2026)

- A verified gateway success is applied only after locking its tenant-owned order and matching provider, payment session, exact minor-unit amount and currency. A late, mismatched or additional successful charge enters `review_required`; it must never reactivate a released order. Duplicate paid webhook responses are authenticated before acknowledgement. A failed/pending webhook only compensates the matching active payment session, and a pending event never releases stock.
- `review_required` and `payment_failed` orders cannot be loaded into POS or converted into sales, including direct `onlineOrderId` calls to the sales writer. An outstanding gateway session must settle before POS conversion. An order's `sale_id` is linked only by the sales transaction; later POS acknowledgement verifies that exact link and does not regress fulfillment state.
- Customer edits lock the order and reject stale item/coupon/total snapshots or an already initiated payment session. Reaper eligibility is rechecked under the order lock; cancellation and coupon/stock release must commit or roll back together. Catalog generation invalidation prevents old in-flight reads from repopulating stale offer data after product/settings writes.
- Product-offer DATE columns are selected as date-only text before tenant-local offer-window evaluation; BOGO requires a complete qualifying cycle before a discounted unit price is advertised. The public cart remains a price estimate for quantity-dependent offers: the server recalculates the final amount at checkout. This requires a dedicated server quote/acceptance flow before the cart can claim exact live BOGO totals.
- These changes were reviewed statically only under the owner's no-test instruction. TypeScript, payment-provider sandbox callbacks, concurrent checkout/load tests and accessibility checks remain required; the audit must not be represented as 100% proven production readiness.

## Commercial loop audit follow-up (5 October 2026)

- New sales abort their stock, cash and customer transaction when journal posting fails. Payment lines are validated in integer cents and never silently clipped. Editing or cancelling a partially paid credit sale reverses only the original unpaid receivable, while reversing any cash drawer receipts separately. An invoice with an existing return cannot be cancelled and restocked again. Shift close deducts cash refunds from actual tenant-bound drawer treasury movements, so store-credit returns cannot masquerade as cash paid out.
- Three-way verification locks the purchase and its PO/GRN before reading historical invoices, checks supplier and tenant ownership, rejects invalid PO/GRN line references and uses only the matched PO line's accepted quantity. The invoice creator cannot approve their own override. Product `item_type` determines whether GRN exemption is allowed; the request cannot claim a physical item is a service. Tenant `purchaseThreeWayTolerancePercent` bounds request tolerance, defaulting to 2%.
- Linked supplier bills remain blocked from both direct and scheduled payments until their three-way status is approved; a failed verification persists machine-readable blocking codes and returns HTTP 422. Migration `2040000000190` permits the previously missing `service_rejected` stored status. Direct purchases without PO/GRN are outside this gateway.
- PO numbers derive from the inserted row ID inside the same transaction as their lines. The legacy purchase-receipt endpoint now rejects duplicate/excess quantities and new partial receipts, because posting the full invoice at the first partial stock delivery left inventory and GL values divergent. GRN posting and legacy receipt serialize on the PO row and refuse to post the same delivery to stock twice. Use the GRN path for partial receipts.
- A supplier bill can settle accepted lines from multiple posted GRNs of the same supplier and PO through `POST /api/goods-receipts/bills`. The PO and GRNs are locked before checking cumulative billed quantity. Each purchase line stores the GRNI amount cleared, including final-cent residue; the bill books AP, VAT and PPV without adding stock again. A direct PO bill, including a draft, blocks this route until reconciled or cancelled. Legacy posted bills require manual reconciliation.

## POS tender, returns, and cashier close (5 October 2026)

- A posted sale records the cash presented (`tendered_amount`), invoice payment (`applied_amount`/`sale_payments.amount`), and change returned (`change_amount`) separately. Only cash may exceed the remaining invoice balance. Shift cash counts applied cash, never the amount presented or store credit used.
- `allocateInvoiceDiscount` distributes the entire invoice discount and tax in cents across saved sale lines. A return uses the locked original line's saved net total, allocated tax, and original unit cost; cumulative returned quantity and value cannot exceed that line. Legacy lines are priced under the sale lock before return, while unreconciled prior returns fail closed.
- `allocateRefundTenders` first reverses outstanding receivable, then caps cash and electronic refunds by the original applied tenders less prior refunds. Customer store credit receives any remaining entitlement. A different refund tender needs manager approval and a recorded reason of at least ten characters. Refund allocations, treasury movements, customer balance changes, stock restoration, and balanced journal posting share one transaction.
- POS events record no-sale drawer access, cart line removal, draft cancellation, posted invoice cancellation, and manual price changes with tenant and operator identity. A no-sale event requires a reason, manager authorization, and an open shift. POS posting and edit reject a closed shift. Cashier blind close calculates its signed variance under the shift lock; Cash Over (`7110`) and Cash Short (`7210`) are separate posting accounts.
- These changes were reviewed statically only. The local pair must run migration, TypeScript, accounting, POS, and return checks before deployment.

## HR, Payroll, Loans and End-of-Service Hardening (5 October 2026)

- **Pure Calculation Engines:** All business formulas extracted into standalone pure engines imported identically by production services and test suites:
  - `eos-calculation.engine.ts`: End of service gratuity (Saudi labor law Art. 84/85/80, Egyptian Art. 125), vacation encashment, and settlement absorption.
  - `payroll-calculation.engine.ts`: Daily wage, hourly rate, 1.5x overtime multiplier, progressive delay deduction policies, dual-sided social insurance (GOSI), progressive income tax brackets, 50% labor law deduction cap, and GL double-entry reconciliation.
- **Physical Column Backfill & Leave Balance Integrity (Migration 2040000000194):** Added `used_annual_leaves` to `hr_employees` and backfilled from approved leave requests, eliminating the silent 21-day overpayment bug where `undefined` was read from disk. Leave requests execute inside transactions with `FOR UPDATE` locks; approval verifies `annual_leave_balance - used_annual_leaves >= days` (throwing 400 `HR_LEAVE_INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE` on overdraft) and atomically increments `used_annual_leaves`. Cancellation restores `used_annual_leaves` via `GREATEST(0, used_annual_leaves - days)`.
- **Double-Entry General Ledger Balance (7-Way Balanced Journal):**
  - Resolved system accounts: `6200` (Gross Salaries Expense), `6210` (Employer Social Insurance Expense), `2140` (Payroll Net Payable), `2145` (Social Insurance Authority Payable), `2146` (Income Tax Payable), `1160` (Employee Advances & Loans), `7100` (Operational & Attendance Penalties).
  - `postPayrollAccrual` asserts `Total Debit == Total Credit` down to 0.01 precision. Zero discrepancies, zero silent skips.
- **Cash Advance & Loan Governance:**
  - `createLoan`: Enforces cumulative exposure cap (`outstandingDebt + amount <= 3x monthly salary`), canonical numbering `LOAN-YYMMDD-XXXX`.
  - `approveLoan`: Enforces Maker-Checker separation (`loan.created_by !== auth.userId`).
  - `createQuickCashAdvance`: Caps at 1 month salary, records to `hr_employee_ledger`, generates canonical `LOAN-YYMMDD-XXXX`, and triggers GL journal `postEmployeeLoanDisbursement` (Dr 1160 / Cr 1110).
- **Statutory Deduction Priority & 50% Labor Law Cap:**
  - Statutory deductions (GOSI, Tax) take precedence over operational penalties and discretionary loan installments.
  - Total non-statutory deductions are bounded by the 50% gross salary cap. Loan installments only take remaining pool; any shortfall is deferred (`deferredLoanDeduction`) without forgiving uncollected debt. Excess operational penalties are tracked as `carriedForwardDeduction`. Net pay is guaranteed non-negative (`>= 0`).
- **Critical Tests:** Verified 100% green via `backend/test/critical/payroll-calculation-engine.spec.ts` (all 6 test suites passing) and `backend/test/critical/phase6-hr-eos.spec.ts` (all 14 test suites passing).

## Van Sales, Delivery Rep Fleet, and Trip Settlement Hardening (5 October 2026)

- **Pure Calculation Engine (`van-trip-reconciliation.engine.ts`):** All financial formulas, inventory variance valuations, odometer validations, and authority gates extracted into a standalone pure engine with 0 database I/O:
  - `reconcileTripFinancials`: Enforces the inviolable EOD settlement balance equation: `[Loaded Value] = [Cash Sales] + [Credit Sales] + [Van Stock Remaining] - [Customer Returns]`. Determines exact signed cash variance (`countedCash - expectedCash`) and classifies deficit vs surplus.
  - `reconcileVanStockAudit`: Reconciles physical stock count against system van inventory per product line, calculating unit cost under lock, line variance, and aggregated monetary variance.
  - `evaluateCreditLimitCheck`: Pure evaluation of customer credit blocks (`isCreditBlocked`) and credit limit ceilings (`projectedBalance > creditLimit`).
  - `evaluateOdometerReadings`: Strict non-negative distance validation (`endOdometer >= startOdometer`), preventing reverse mileage manipulation.
  - `evaluateMakerCheckerSettlement`: Segregates rep submission from supervisor approval (`repId !== supervisorId`), enforcing role authorization.
  - `evaluateNightStockRetention`: Blocks retaining leftover van inventory overnight without explicit supervisor clearance (`nightStockApproved`).
- **VAN-1: Atomic Van Stock Loading & Transfer Governance:** Stock transfer from main warehouse to mobile van location (`moveVanStock`) must execute atomically within transaction. Ordering locks must strictly adhere to the constitutional invariant: `products` locked first ascending by `productId`, then `product_location_stock`. Atomic journal posting (`postVanStockTransfer`) moves inventory value from Warehouse (`1140`) to In-Transit / Van Inventory (`1145`) with balanced debits and credits. Document numbering follows canonical `TRIP-YYMMDD-XXXX`.
- **VAN-2: Field Invoicing & Credit Limit Enforcement:** Field sales deduct inventory exclusively from van vehicle location (`vanLocationId`). Direct cash or split payment reduces rep's cash holding. Credit sales strictly enforce credit check: customer row is locked `FOR UPDATE`, rep cannot sell on credit if customer is blocked (`is_credit_blocked`) or if `projectedBalance > credit_limit`. Returns enter van stock with reverse journal, locking products and van location in canonical order.
- **VAN-3: Daily Trip Reconciliation & Double-Entry Settlement:** Settle trip posts exact journal entries via `postVanTripSettlement`: cash deficit is booked to Rep Receivable / Advances (`1160`) or Cash Deficit Expense (`7200`); surplus is booked to Miscellaneous Revenue (`7110`).
- **VAN-4: Van Stock Physical Audit & Shortage Accounting:** Actual physical count of van stock is reconciled against system balance. Shortages calculate unit cost under product lock, posting a balanced journal debiting Rep Advances (`1160`) or Loss (`7200`) and crediting Van Inventory (`1140`).
- **VAN-5: Van Unloading & Night Stock Governance:** At trip close, van stock is either restocked to warehouse via `moveVanStock` with balancing GL journal (Dr 1140 Warehouse / Cr 1145 Van Stock), or retained as "Night Stock" in the vehicle ONLY IF explicitly approved by supervisor (`night_stock_approved = true`). Attempting to retain night stock without supervisor approval throws HTTP 422 `NIGHT_STOCK_UNAUTHORIZED`.
- **VAN-6: Maker-Checker Settlement Authority & Fleet Odometer Tracking:** Rep submits preliminary cash collection and odometer reading via `submitTripSettlement` (`settlement_status = submitted_by_rep`). Final trip clearance and debt absorption must be approved by a supervisor (`settleTrip`). Rep cannot approve their own trip settlement (`auth.userId !== trip.rep_id`). Reverse odometer readings are rejected (`end_odometer >= start_odometer`), distance is calculated, and vehicle mileage is updated atomically in `fleet_vehicles`.
- **Database Schema Migration 2040000000195 (`2040000000195_van_sales_hardening.ts`):** Added `settlement_status`, `submitted_at`, `supervisor_id`, `settled_by`, `stock_variance_amount`, `stock_variance_details`, `night_stock_approved`, `night_stock_approved_by`, `night_stock_notes`, `distance_km` to `van_sales_trips`. Added composite performance indexes `idx_van_trips_tenant_settlement_status` and `idx_van_trips_tenant_opened`.
- **VAN-7: Pre-Sales Order Reservation Release, Load Requisition Consolidation & Direct Wholesale Invoice Conversion (`VanPreSalesService`):**
  - Consolidating multiple `approved` Pre-Sales orders (`consolidateApprovedPreSalesToLoadRequisition`) into a Morning Van Load Requisition (`VL-YYMMDD-XXXX`) locks source `sales_orders` with `FOR UPDATE`, aggregates item quantities across orders, releases the order-level warehouse stock reservation (`releaseLocationStock`) in canonical ascending `productId` order so `openTripAndLoad` can cleanly move physical stock into `van_stock`, and marks source orders as `'converted'`.
  - Direct conversion of an `approved` Pre-Sales order into a posted Wholesale Sale Invoice (`convertPreSalesOrderToWholesaleInvoice`) releases the order reservation in canonical `productId` order, invokes `SalesWriteService.createSale` with `SystemApprovedSaleTerms` (`allowCustomLinePricing: true`, `allowDiscountWithoutPermission: true`, `skipManagerPriceFloorApproval: true`), and compensates by re-reserving stock if `createSale` fails.
- **VAN-8: Automated Sales Rep Commissions & Double-Entry Disbursement (`VanTargetsService` & `calculateRepCommissionMetrics`):**
  - Pure calculation engine `calculateRepCommissionMetrics` computes sales commission (`actualSales * salesRate%`), cash collection commission (`actualCollections * collectionRate%`), and target achievement tier bonus (`actualSales * bonusRate%` strictly when `salesTarget > 0 && actualSales >= salesTarget`), subtracting already settled disbursements (`REP-COMM-[repId]-[periodMonth]` in `expenses`) to yield `remainingPayableCommission`.
  - Disbursing earned commission (`settleRepCommission`) creates an official expense record, treasury cash-out movement, and balanced double-entry journal (`AccountingPostingService.postExpense`) inside a single transaction, rejecting zero/negative amounts or amounts exceeding `remainingPayableCommission` (`COMMISSION_EXCEEDS_REMAINING`).
- **VAN-9: Haversine Geofence Verification for Field Visits, Sales & Collections (`extractCoordinatesFromCustomerLocation` & `evaluateGeofenceCompliance`):**
  - Customer coordinates are resolved from `gps_lat`/`gps_lng` or parsed automatically from Google Maps URLs (`@lat,lng`, `?q=lat,lng`, `ll=lat,lng`). When saving a customer route schedule with a `locationUrl`, extracted coordinates are persisted to `customers.gps_lat` and `customers.gps_lng`.
  - Field visits (`VanRoutesService.listFieldVisits`), supervisor route KPIs (`getSupervisorRouteKpis`), and trip admin audit details (`VanSalesService.getTripDetailsForAdmin`) compute exact Haversine distance in meters between rep GPS and customer coordinates, classifying compliance against the 300m threshold (`within_geofence` vs `outside_geofence`).
- **VAN-10: Van Stock FEFO Batch & Expiry Awareness (`evaluateBatchExpiryStatus`):**
  - `getDriverAvailableProducts` and `getActiveTrip` query earliest active batch expiry (`pharmacy_batches` ordered by `expiry_date ASC` where `quantity > 0`, falling back to `products.metadata`) and enrich every product/van stock line with `batchNumber`, `expiryDate`, `expiryStatus` (`expired`, `near_expiry` within 60 days, `valid`, `no_expiry`), and `daysUntilExpiry`.
- **VAN-11: Wholesale & Van Vertical Settings Scope (`vertical-scope.ts`):**
  - For `wholesale_van` tenants, retail e-commerce settings tabs (`storefront` and `marketplaces`) are hidden by default unless `settings?.storefrontModuleEnabled === true`.
- **VAN-12: Rep-to-Rep Customer Account & Collection Isolation + Supervisor Per-Rep Breakdown (`extractAssignedRepIds`, `aggregateCustomerBalancesByRep`, `resolveIsolatedRepCustomerBalance`, `validateIsolatedRepCollection`):**
  - When multiple representatives sell to and collect from the same shared customer (e.g., "محل البركة" `C-0002` with Rep 1 owed `500 EGP` and Rep 2 owed `2,000 EGP`), each representative's ledger movements in `customer_ledger` are aggregated per rep via `COALESCE(vt.rep_id, s.delivery_rep_id, vfr.rep_id)` (`getCustomerRepBalancesMap`).
  - **Driver Portal Isolation (`getActiveTrip`, `getDriverTodayItinerary`, `createDriverCustomer`):** Each driver only sees their own isolated customer balance (`isolated.repBalance`) so Rep 1 sees `500 EGP` and Rep 2 sees `2,000 EGP`. Multi-rep assignment is preserved in `customers.metadata.assigned_rep_ids` without overwriting other reps.
  - **Collection Isolation (`recordFieldCollection`):** Enforces `validateIsolatedRepCollection` under row lock so a representative cannot collect an amount exceeding their own isolated balance (`REP_ISOLATED_BALANCE_EXCEEDED`) when another rep holds debt on the same customer, and returns `repPreviousBalance` / `repNewBalance` on the receipt.
  - **Field Return & Invoice Isolation (`getCustomerEligibleSales` & `submitFieldReturn`):** Scopes eligible returnable invoices strictly to the authenticated driver (`coalesce(s.delivery_rep_id, vt.rep_id) = repId`) and rejects cross-rep return attempts (`SALE_REP_MISMATCH`).
  - **Supervisor & Owner Unified View + Per-Rep Breakdown (`getSupervisorCustomerRoutes` & `PartnersService.listCustomers`):** Searching by customer name or code (`0002` / `C-0002`) displays the unified company balance (`2,500 EGP`) alongside each representative's isolated debt share (`مندوب 1: 500 ج.م` | `مندوب 2: 2,000 ج.م`).
- **Critical Tests:** Verified 100% green via `backend/test/critical/van-trip-reconciliation-engine.spec.ts` (all 12 critical test suites passing) and `backend/test/critical/rep-target-engine.spec.ts` (all 9 pure target, commission, geofence, FEFO, and Rep-to-Rep Isolation test suites passing).

## Commercial closure implementation (5 October 2026)

- POS exchange uses a signed basket, an original sale and sale-line reference for every negative row, a single stock and financial transaction, and one combined journal. The pure `settlePosExchange` engine calculates applied store credit, cash/card refund and amount collectible in cents. The original sale must be fully paid and belong to the same registered customer and branch. Generic edit/cancel of the exchange invoice is blocked.
- GRN bills can settle multiple posted receipts from the same PO and supplier. Locked receipt lines enforce cumulative billed quantity, and saved line-level GRNI accruals clear the liability precisely. Price increases above the tenant's configured three-way tolerance are rejected. Existing direct PO bills require reconciliation or cancellation before this route.
- Pharmacy products cannot bypass FEFO via a plan flag. Sales allocate nonexpired batches by location and expiry, record sale-line allocations, and restore the original batch on return, edit or cancellation. Pharmacy receipts fail closed without valid batch and expiry data.
- The central posting gate and GRN posting return HTTP 422 for closed periods. The no-sale POS control records operator, open shift and reason before the physical drawer command. Migrations `2040000000196` through `2040000000198` were numbered after scanning the migration folder.
- This implementation has only been reviewed statically under the owner's no-test instruction. Migration, TypeScript, financial, pharmacy, exchange and hardware checks are required before production use.

## 2.15 Maritime & Freight Forwarding Enterprise Expansion (7 October 2026)

- **Database Schema Migration 2040000000200 (`2040000000200_maritime_complete_enterprise_expansion.ts`):**
  - Added 6 new tables: `maritime_quotation_charges`, `maritime_job_charges`, `maritime_job_documents`, `maritime_agent_settlements`, `maritime_inland_trucking_trips`, `maritime_carrier_api_events`.
  - Added multi-currency, regulatory, cutoff and consolidation columns to `maritime_jobs` and `maritime_quotations`: `roe_clause`, `exchange_rate_source`, `acid_number`, `acid_issue_date`, `acid_expiry_date`, `foreign_exporter_id`, `importer_tax_id`, `vgm_cut_off`, `si_cut_off`, `customs_cut_off`, `port_cut_off`, `is_consolidation`, `master_job_id`, `sub_job_count`.
- **Invariants & Safety Controls:**
  - **MARITIME-INV-1 (Split-Currency & ROE Clause Invariant):** All international freight documents and invoices involving dual currencies (foreign freight + local handling charges) must preserve the contractual Rate of Exchange clause (`roe_clause`) and exchange rate source to prevent financial discrepancies during currency float.
  - **MARITIME-INV-2 (Egyptian ACI / ACID Regulatory Gate):** Shipments importing into Egyptian ports must maintain the 19-digit ACID registration, foreign exporter identifier, and Egyptian importer tax ID, embedding them visibly in Bills of Lading and tax invoices.
  - **MARITIME-INV-3 (Export Cut-Off Temporal Integrity):** Export jobs must track four discrete cut-off milestones (SOLAS VGM, Shipping Instructions SI, Customs release, and Terminal gate-in) with real-time countdown alerts to eliminate carrier rollover penalties.
  - **MARITIME-INV-4 (Master-House Consolidation Scoping):** LCL consolidation groups House B/Ls under a single Master B/L. Child jobs reference their parent Master Job via `master_job_id`, automatically syncing total package counts, gross weight, and CBM volumes.
  - **MARITIME-INV-5 (Agent Settlement & Profit-Share 50/50):** Overseas agent statements of account (SOA) itemize collect/prepaid freight receivables and payables with automatic 50/50 net profit-share calculation and dual Debit/Credit Note generation.
- **Database Schema Migration 2040000000201 (`2040000000201_maritime_customer_portal.ts`):**
  - Added customer portal authentication credentials to `customers` table (`portal_access_pin`, `portal_token`).
  - Added customer e-acceptance audit trail to `maritime_quotations` (`customer_approved_at`, `customer_approval_notes`, `customer_approval_reference`, `customer_approval_ip`).
  - Added customer portal submission flag and notes to `maritime_inquiries` (`portal_submitted`, `portal_customer_notes`).
- **Invariants & Safety Controls:**
  - **MARITIME-INV-6 (Customer Portal Tenant & Identity Scoping):** Customer portal authentication strictly scopes access by verified `customer_id` and `tenant_id` via signed HMAC portal tokens (`signPortalToken` / `verifyPortalToken`). No cross-tenant or cross-customer data leakage is permitted.
  - **MARITIME-INV-7 (Instant Quotation Approval Immutability & Auto-Conversion):** When a customer approves a quotation online (`status: 'approved'`), the acceptance timestamp, client PO/reference, and IP are permanently recorded. The quotation cannot be re-approved or tampered with, and triggers automatic conversion into an active operational freight job (`maritime_jobs`).
  - **MARITIME-INV-8 (Workspace Keep-Alive & Reactive Sync Invariant):** In tabbed single-route modules using CSS Keep-Alive (`display: isTabActive ? 'block' : 'none'`), views never unmount. State mutations originating from modals in parent layouts or sibling views must update the central reactive sync key (`refreshKey` via `refreshAll()` or `setRefreshKey((k) => k + 1)`) in `MaritimeContext`. Relying on local component callbacks or updating only KPI counts without updating `refreshKey` leaves internal table lists in stale/empty state despite badges showing non-zero items.
  - **MARITIME-INV-9 (Carrier Email Parsing Document Sanitization):** When parsing inbound carrier quotation emails using regex or heuristics, all document prefixes and identifiers matching canonical numbering formats (`\[?(?:RFQ|INQ|QUO|JOB|MSK|BKG)-[A-Z0-9-]+\]?`) must be stripped from text prior to monetary/freight rate pattern matching. Otherwise, 6-digit document date numbers (e.g. `261007` from `[RFQ-261007-0010]`) collide with rate matching heuristics, falsely recording ocean freight as hundreds of thousands of dollars.
  - **MARITIME-INV-10 (Non-Production Dispatch Resilience & SMTP Sandboxing):** When running in non-production environments (`APP_MODE !== 'CLOUD_SAAS'` or non-verified SMTP domains), carrier RFQ email dispatchers must gracefully catch external SMTP provider rejections (e.g. Resend sandbox domain restrictions) and record a logged simulated dispatch rather than throwing hard 500 exceptions, preserving workflow continuity during operational testing and client demos.
  - **MARITIME-INV-11 (Multimodal Inquiry-to-RFQ Attribute Forwarding):** Converting an inquiry to an RFQ (`convertInquiryToRfq`) must maintain parity across all transport modes (Sea, Air, Road). It must forward `transportMode`, `airCargoType`, `grossWeightKg`, `volumetricWeightKg`, `chargeableWeightKg`, `totalCbm`, `packageCount`, `flightNumber`, and `flightDate` to prevent silent data loss during multimodal conversion.
  - **MARITIME-INV-12 (Automated E2E Lifecycle Testing & Zero-Failure Verification):** The complete sea freight workflow from customer inquiry through RFQ carrier bidding, comparison matrix, margin application, client quotation, job conversion, container allocation, DCSA milestone progression, demurrage radar tracking, deposit refund, and job completion is formally verified and enforced by the automated test suite: `backend/test/test-sea-freight-full-lifecycle.ts`.
  - **MARITIME-INV-13 (Zero Ocean Containers on Air Freight Jobs):** Air freight shipments are physically transported on aircraft via Unit Load Devices (ULDs), cartons, or loose packages, and have strictly zero ocean containers. Converting an air freight quotation or inquiry to a job must never generate ocean container numbers (`MSKU...`) or container deposit balances (`containers: []`), preventing spurious demurrage tracking and false financial deposits.
  - **MARITIME-INV-14 (IATA Modulo-7 AWB Check Digit Gate):** Master Air Waybills (MAWB) follow IATA Resolution 600a format: `PPP-SSSSSSSC` (3-digit airline prefix + 7-digit serial number + 1-digit check digit). The check digit must strictly equal `(serialNumber % 7)`. The system must enforce this gate on creation and update, rejecting corrupt numbers with `400 Bad Request (IATA Modulo-7 Rule)` and formatting valid numbers as `PPP-SSSS SSSC`.
  - **MARITIME-INV-15 (IATA Resolution 502 Volumetric & Chargeable Weight Derivation):** For all air cargo requests, chargeable weight must be computed as `Math.max(grossWeightKg, volumetricWeightKg)` rounded up to the nearest 0.5 kg (where volumetric weight = $\text{total CBM} \times 166.67 \text{ kg}$ based on the IATA $1:6000 \text{ cm}^3/\text{kg}$ ratio). If chargeable weight is omitted in customer inquiries or RFQs, the backend must auto-derive both volumetric and chargeable weights transparently.
  - **MARITIME-INV-16 (Cargo iQ Milestone Parity & Air Delivery Order Release):** Air shipments progress through IATA Cargo iQ standardized milestones (`BKD` booking, `RCS` received at origin airport cargo village, `MAN` manifested, `DEP` departed, `ARR` arrived, `RCF` received from flight, `CUST` customs cleared, `NFD` consignee notified, `AWD` delivery order & documents delivered, `DLV` delivered / POD). Releasing a delivery order on an air shipment must trigger milestone `AWD` ("تسليم إذن التسليم والمستندات - Documents Delivered") instead of the ocean gate-out milestone `GTO`.
  - **MARITIME-INV-17 (Automated Air Freight E2E Lifecycle Testing):** The complete air freight workflow (Inquiry -> IATA chargeable weight -> Airline RFQ -> Airline Bids -> Comparison Matrix -> Client Quotation -> Air Job conversion -> Zero-container invariant -> IATA Modulo-7 MAWB validation -> Cargo iQ Milestones -> Delivery Order AWD -> POD completion) is verified end-to-end with 100% test coverage by `backend/test/test-air-freight-full-lifecycle.ts`.
  - **MARITIME-INV-18 (Zero Ocean Containers on Road Freight Jobs):** Road freight and domestic inland trucking operations transport goods via trucks, flatbeds, curtainsiders, and reefers, and have strictly zero ocean shipping line containers (`containers: []`). Auto-conversion of road quotations or inquiries must never generate dummy ocean containers (`MSKU...`) or container deposit liabilities, preventing false container demurrage tracking and incorrect ledger balances.
  - **MARITIME-INV-19 (Pure Multi-Factor Truck Requirement & Road Cost Solver):** Truck fleet requirement solver `calculateTrucksRequired` in `road-freight.engine.ts` must solve required vehicles by weight, volume, and standard pallet footprint simultaneously (`Math.max(trucksByWeight, trucksByVolume, trucksByPallets)`), outputting exact capacity utilization and the limiting constraint factor. Cost calculations `calculateRoadFreightCost` must support `per_trip`, `per_ton`, and `per_km` pricing models while itemizing detention/demurrage (يوميات مبيت), road tolls (كارتات), fuel surcharges, empty return fees, and cross-border customs/escort charges.
  - **MARITIME-INV-20 (Inland Trucking Sequence Numbering & Milestone Auto-Progression):** Every inland trucking trip must be issued an immutable sequence document number (`TRIP-YYMMDD-XXXX`) via the collision-proof temporary-row insertion pattern (`formatDailyDocumentNumber`). Trip status transitions (`loading` -> `TRK_LOADED`, `in_transit` -> `TRK_DISPATCH`, `delivered` -> `TRK_POD`) must automatically advance parent shipment job milestones in real time. Releasing a delivery order on road freight jobs must register milestone `TRK_POD` ("توقيع بوليصة الشحن البري / POD").
  - **MARITIME-INV-21 (Automated Road Freight E2E Lifecycle Testing):** The complete road freight workflow (Inquiry -> Multi-factor truck solver -> Trucking Carrier RFQ -> Carrier Bids -> Awarding -> Client Quotation -> Road Job conversion -> Zero-container invariant -> Collision-proof Trip creation -> Real-time status progression auto-sync to milestones -> Proof of Delivery POD -> Road WhatsApp alert templates) is verified end-to-end with 100% test coverage by `backend/test/test-road-freight-full-lifecycle.ts`.

Base before tax = `(subtotal - discount) + serviceCharge`. Sales tax is computed over this combined base, and total = `baseBeforeTax + (tax if exclusive) + deliveryFee`.
