Also unchanged: the settleTrip expectedCash derivation (grossCashCollected minus totalTripExpenses, pre-rounded with toFixed(2) -- a separate finding, not a variance computation), the +/-0.01 ledger thresholds and the -0.5 suspicion threshold (policy bands, not precision), and journal amounts (postVanTripSettlement re-derives its own journal variance at book digits from expected/counted cash). Prospective only: variances already stored under the inline form keep their stored values; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-PURCHASE-ORDER-TOTALS-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): purchase-order totals -- line totals, subtotal, discount, tax and grand total -- are computed by exactly one function, calculatePurchaseOrderTotals(items, discountAmount, digits) in purchases/engines/purchase-order-totals.engine.ts, rounding once with roundMoney at the tenant's book digits; digits are REQUIRED and never silently defaulted to 2, so a 3-decimal book stores its true totals (a KWD line total of 100.005 stays 100.005; the old hardcoded-cents form wrote 100.01) and 2-decimal books are unchanged. createOrder resolves digits on the pool handle before its transaction and updateOrder on the transaction handle (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits); the stored totals are the approval amount and the convertToBill reconciliation input, and convertToBill recomputes its gate subtotal at book digits with the 0.01 tolerance unchanged. Client-supplied totals in the DTO are never read. Deliberately unchanged: the RFQ-award totals (unrounded sums rounded only at write, no discount pro-rating) and the reorder-estimate totals (fixed 3 decimals, no tax) write the same columns with different arithmetic and need separate decisions; the 0.01 conversion tolerance is a policy band, not precision. Prospective only: totals already stored under the cents form keep their stored values; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-PUBLIC-API-CROSS-ORIGIN (1 November 2026): the public endpoints under /api/public/ (pricing catalog, trial signup) are readable from the marketing site origin https://zsystemai.com regardless of the CORS_ORIGINS env list: resolvePublicApiCorsOrigin in backend/src/common/utils/public-cors-origin.util.ts returns the requesting origin only on an exact match with the marketing origin or an entry of the configured CORS_ORIGINS list, and false otherwise (no Access-Control-Allow-Origin header, so the browser blocks the read); main.ts branches on the /api/public/ path before the generic CORS callback, and the security-headers middleware sets Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: cross-origin on those paths instead of same-origin (the storefront media precedent) so the CORP gate cannot block a CORS-allowed read. Deliberately unchanged: the CORS_ORIGINS behaviour for every other route (including the /api/updates/ echo), all other security headers, and the public endpoints themselves (no pricing or signup logic changed). Prospective only: deployments whose CORS_ORIGINS already listed the marketing origin see no behaviour change; the single pricing endpoint remains the one source of prices for the website and the in-app client - no bundled or stale price fallback is reintroduced. Live verification after deploy is a browser fetch from https://zsystemai.com plus a curl with Origin: https://zsystemai.com asserting Access-Control-Allow-Origin: https://zsystemai.com on GET /api/public/pricing-catalog.
- INV-MARITIME-SETTLE-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): the customer-balance settle of a maritime job -- the unpaid remainder and the accumulated paid total in settleJobFromCustomerBalance -- is rounded once with roundMoney at the tenant's book digits, resolved on the transaction handle (tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits, the pattern this file already uses); the stored client_paid_total (NUMERIC(15,4)) keeps the third decimal on a 3-decimal book (invoiced 100.005 settles as 100.005, not 100.01) and 2-decimal books are unchanged for ordinary amounts. The Math.max(0, ...) guard, the amount cap tolerance (+0.001), and the no-journal behavior (milestone note only; the invoice and receipt already changed AR) are unchanged. Deliberately unchanged: the 0.01 `full` band (a 0.005 remainder still marks the job paid -- a policy band, separate decision) and the sibling writer in purchases-write.service.ts, which stores the same column as an unrounded float sum and needs its own convergence decision. Prospective only: previously stored paid totals keep their stored values; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-FISCAL-YEAR-CLOSE-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): the fiscal-year close -- preview balances, P&L nets, proposed closing lines and the stored totals -- is computed by exactly one function, buildFiscalYearClosePreview, rounding once with roundMoney at the tenant's book digits (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits); the standalone preview resolves digits on the pool handle, executeFiscalYearClose resolves them on the transaction handle, re-runs the same computation inside its transaction and posts the RECOMPUTED lines, never the preview lines verbatim. Preview and post agree by exact equality at book digits, per account and for the totals (the old 0.009 drift band and the old 0.05 balance band are removed: the close is balanced by construction, so any imbalance after rounding is a real bug); a base-currency/rate mismatch fails closed in both preview and execute. Deliberately unchanged: the 2dp toMoney helper still shapes list/get responses, reopenFiscalYear reverses the stored closing entry verbatim, and the monthly period close is a separate path. Prospective only: already-closed years keep their stored totals; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-OPENING-BALANCE-POSTING-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): the opening-balance computation -- cash/bank openings, balancing capital, operational totals and preview lines -- is computed by exactly one function, buildOpeningBalancesPreview, rounding once with roundMoney at the tenant's book digits (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits); the standalone preview resolves digits on the pool handle, postOpeningBalances resolves them on the transaction handle, re-runs the same computation inside its transaction and posts the RECOMPUTED lines through the canonical posting boundary (postDomainJournal/insertPostedJournal), never by direct journal_entry_lines inserts and never the preview lines verbatim. Preview and post agree by exact equality at book digits, per line and for the totals (the old 0.0001 balance tolerance is removed); a base-currency/rate mismatch fails closed in both preview and post. The boundary's period-lock behavior applies without an admin exemption for the operational lock. Deliberately unchanged: the 2dp toMoney helper still shapes other responses, PartnersService partner openings keep their own pinned path, catalog opening stock and manual journals are separate packages. Prospective only: already-posted opening entries keep their stored lines; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-PUBLIC-PRICING-OFFER-STRICT (1 November 2026): the public single-offer endpoint (GET /api/public/pricing-catalog?product=&country=) resolves strictly through PricingCatalogService.resolvePublicOffer: an unknown product id, an unknown country code, or a blocked country returns null and the controller answers 404 with code pricing_offer_not_found, never HTTP 200 carrying another product's or country's prices. The tenant/internal path resolveForTenant deliberately keeps its retail/EG fallback (an unknown recorded activity must not break the in-app billing screen) and priceForLevel is unchanged; the no-parameter full-catalog path still enumerates only published products and non-blocked countries through resolveForTenant. The shared PublicPricing shape now carries product.sellOffline (from the catalog record, so the offline offer cannot render for products that do not sell offline) and vatIncluded (false, from the catalog source, so clients can state prices exclude VAT). Deliberately unchanged: all prices, floors, addons (Gulf addons stay null — contact-for-price, never invented numbers), perpetual/offline pricing (still source-only, needs a commercial decision before it is served), the 5-minute cache header, and the CORS policy from the public-API cross-origin entry. Prospective only: previously served wrong-product 200s are not rewritten; clients that sent a typo'd id now get a 404 and must correct the id.
- INV-MANUAL-JOURNAL-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): the manual-journal computation -- per-line amounts, totals, the balance band and the 7290 rounding plug -- is computed by exactly one path, createManualJournalEntry, rounding once with roundMoney at the tenant's book digits (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits, resolved on the transaction handle inside writeJournal, fail closed on a base-currency/rate mismatch); the entry posts through the canonical posting boundary (postDomainJournal/insertPostedJournal), never by direct journal_entries/journal_entry_lines inserts and never with a JE-TMP temp number. The balance band is one minor unit and the plug trigger is half a minor unit, the boundary's own maxAllowedDifference/trigger rules (the old 0.05 balance band and 0.001 plug trigger are removed); the manual path keeps its own 7290 plug line with the Arabic rounding-difference description and the boundary backstops it. The boundary's cashFlowCategory passthrough preserves the entry's cash-flow category for the cash-flow report. The manual eligibility checks (manual-entry accounts, control/receivable/payable/inventory accounts, mapped control accounts, tax period, references) stay in the manual path because the boundary checks active accounts only. Deliberately unchanged: the 2dp toMoney helper still shapes other responses, draft journal entries are a separate path, fiscal close (P0-1) and opening balances (P0-2) are separate packages. The shared toBookMoney helper is P0-2's (identical semantics, reused, not duplicated). Prospective only: already-posted manual entries keep their stored lines; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-WITHHOLDING-TAX-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): withholding-tax creation and subledger sync compute at the tenant's book digits (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits, resolved on the transaction handle, fail closed on a base-currency/rate mismatch); createTransaction rounds base and tax once with roundMoney inside its transaction, the remittance posting already re-rounds at book digits, and syncPartnerSubLedger moves the partner balance by the stored tax_amount rounded at book digits with balance_after as the exact returned balance (no 2dp re-rounding anywhere in the create→post→sync chain, so GL and subledger agree exactly). Migration 2040000000276 widens withholding_tax_transactions.base_amount and tax_amount from NUMERIC(15,2) to scale 3, data-preserving, following the 0257/0258 widening pattern — code and migration ship together, because code alone cannot store a third decimal on real Postgres. Deliberately unchanged: extractFromPurchases keeps its raw-settings digits resolution (its spec has no currency_exchange_rates table; the JSON-quoted settings.currency question stays open pending one live check), return/report records keep 2dp display shaping, PDC balance_after is a separate package. Prospective only: already-stored transactions keep their stored amounts; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-VAN-FIELD-SALE-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): the field-sale pricing derivation in executeFieldSaleInternal -- authorized price, unit offer savings, line totals, sale totals, cash discount and the cash/credit split -- rounds once with roundMoney at the tenant's book digits (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits, resolved once on the transaction handle, fail closed on a base-currency/rate mismatch, one hoisted toBookMoney helper shared by the derivation and the return echo); postSale already re-rounds at book digits, so it is idempotent over the stored values and the journal lines it COMPUTES agree exactly with the stored sale with no residual and no 2dp re-rounding in the computation. STORAGE GATE (programme-wide, open, not this package): journal_entry_lines.debit/credit are still NUMERIC(14,2) on real Postgres with no widening migration, so a computed 30.375 stores as 30.38 until a 0258-pattern widening migration lands; the sale-side columns this package writes (sales totals, sale_items unit_price/line_total, customer_ledger, sale_payments, customers.balance) were already widened to scale 3 by migration 0258, and products.cost_price is NUMERIC(20,6) by migration 0252, so the stored-sale agreement is real on Postgres and only the journal leg is gated. The +/-0.05 client-price tolerance STAYS ABSOLUTE: it is a fraud-policy allowance for human price deviation (the Price Integrity Guard), not a rounding artifact, so it does not shrink to one minor unit at 3dp; client prices themselves round at book digits and are compared against the 0.05 band at full precision, and the same absolute 0.05 governs the return guards, whose behavior is unchanged. Deliberately unchanged: the fraud attemptedAmount audit shaping; the overdue SQL gate and the overdueAmount sum (the P1-21 rider is left out: they feed only a >0 check already covered by oldestOverdueDays plus a display string, so the 2dp sum cannot flip the decision); the return guards in van-returns.service.ts and van-trip-reconciliation.engine.ts; the van offer-preview derivation (executeFieldSale re-derives); sale_items.cost_price as the WAC input (postSale rounds the COGS sum at book digits); the credit-check engine's internal 2dp input rounding (separate engine, separate package); the shop-sale analogue calculateAllowedSaleUnitPrice (digits UNVERIFIED, follow-up, not touched). Prospective only: already-stored sales keep their stored amounts; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-JOURNAL-LINES-SCALE-3 (1 November 2026): journal_entry_lines.debit/credit store at three minor units (NUMERIC(15,3), integer capacity preserved) via migration 2040000000277, following the 0257/0258 widening pattern (information_schema guard, skip when already scale >= 3, throw when missing or not a fixed NUMERIC, down is a no-op that never truncates posted history). No code change: the canonical posting boundary already computes every journal line at the tenant's book digits, and its own comment anticipates this widening; code and migration ship together for the journal leg, because code alone could not store a third decimal on real Postgres (a computed 30.375 stored as 30.38). This closes the programme-wide storage gate for every landed posting package (P0-1 fiscal close, P0-2 opening balances, P0-3 manual journals, U1 overhead, U2 cash variance, U3 purchase-order totals, P0-4 withholding tax, P0-5 field sales), whose computed lines now store exactly. Deliberately unchanged: debit/credit are the only amount columns widened (every journal_entry_lines writer touches amounts only through them); already-posted lines keep their stored values (widening preserves them, no history is re-rounded); the WAC purchase-return recompute is a separate package. Prospective only.
- INV-PURCHASE-RETURN-WAC-RECOMPUTE (1 November 2026): a purchase return keeps the original-cost credit (INV-PURCHASE-RETURN-ORIGINAL-COST) and recomputes the product WAC in the SAME transaction: createPurchaseReturn calls the canonical writer revalueStockReturn (inventory-valuation.ts, beside valueStockReceipt) right after applyStockDelta, while the product row lock is still held; the writer recovers the prior quantity from applyStockDelta's corrected globalAfter (INV-WAC-QUANTITY-SOURCE) and re-blends with weightedReturnCost, the mirror of weightedReceiptCost in the canonical engine file (same blend identity, same 6dp rounding, same degenerate policy — no second formula). The subtracted value is the line's GL inventory credit at book digits (sold qty x original per-sold-unit cost), exactly what postPurchaseReturn sums from return_items, so cost_price x afterQty reconciles with the 1140 leg; the diagnosed example is 17 units at WAC 15 returning 1 at original cost 10 -> stock 16, cost_price 15.3125 (245/16), 5190 stays 0. postPurchaseReturn is deliberately untouched (its priceVariance stays 0 by construction under original-cost credit, and recovery re-posts must never reprice stock twice). Degenerate pools keep beforeCost: an emptied pool keeps the last booked cost, and a return whose value exceeds the pool value never stores a negative unit cost nor reprices the remainder up to the returned cost. FOLLOW-UP (gated, not this package): the live-Postgres acceptance expectation for 1140 (240 -> 245) changes only in the acceptance-fixture step owned elsewhere, after DB-leg inspection. Prospective only: already-posted returns keep their stored cost_price; no history is re-blended.
- INV-CUSTOMER-INSTALLMENTS-MINOR-UNITS (1 November 2026): createPlan and the payment/progress/summary money amounts in customer-installments.service.ts (live path backend/src/modules/sales/services/, not the gap report's services/ path) compute at the tenant's book digits (P2 pattern: tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits, resolved on the transaction handle inside createPlan/payInstallment and on the db handle for reads, fail closed on a base-currency/rate mismatch); plan totals round once with roundMoney (sub-minor inputs round leniently, never throw) and the schedule splits in minor units through the pure helper splitInstallmentSchedule in installment-interest.engine.ts (book digits required, never a silent default to 2), with the LAST row absorbing the remainder — the same last-row shape as the supplier schedule builder and allocateInstallmentInterest — so schedule rows sum exactly to totalWithInterest and allocateInstallmentInterest's reconcile check (payments sum == principal + interest in minor units) holds; the diagnosed example is a KWD plan of 100.126 over 3 rows [33.375, 33.375, 33.376] (monthly_amount 33.375, the floored share), and a 100 over 3 EGP plan [33.33, 33.33, 33.34]. SHAPE CHANGE (deliberate): the old cents split gave the remainder to the FIRST rows; the new split moves it to the last row, and the count guard is now at least one minor unit per installment (same INVALID_INSTALLMENT_COUNT code, updated wording; no spec asserted the old 'one cent' wording). NO MIGRATION: customer_installment_plans/customer_installments amount columns are already NUMERIC(15,4) (migration 2040000000027), which stores a third decimal exactly. Deliberately unchanged: allocateInstallmentInterest and postCustomerInstallmentInterest (already book digits), the interest scheduler/accrual path (no money math), frontend display (formatCurrency already resolves decimals per currency — display shaping unverified in this package), integer progress percents. Prospective only: already-posted plans keep their stored amounts and schedules; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-CATALOG-STOCK-TOTALS-BOOK-DIGITS (1 November 2026): every catalog stock_movements.total_cost row and the opening-stock journal leg come from ONE computation, stockMovementTotal(qty, unitCost, bookDigits) in catalog/engines/stock-movement-total.engine.ts (book digits required, never a silent default to 2; sub-minor products round leniently at book digits): postOpeningStock posts the helper amount through postDomainJournal, the two opening-balance inserts store the same helper total beside their postOpeningStock call in the same transaction, and the zero-removed-stock deduct, fashion-matrix adjustment, and archive stock-zeroing legs store the helper total that postInventoryAdjustment reads verbatim (re-rounded at book digits) — so the stored total and the journal leg can never diverge by rounding; the diagnosed example is 3 x 33.375 = 100.125 stored and posted at 100.125 on a 3dp tenant (was 100.13), and 16 x 15.3125 = 245. Digits resolve on the caller's own transaction handle via tenantBaseCurrency + currencyMoneyDigits, fail closed on a base-currency/rate mismatch (settings.currency is parsed JSON-quoted-or-bare by tenantBaseCurrency). STORAGE VERIFIED: stock_movements.total_cost NUMERIC(18,3) and unit_cost NUMERIC(16,3) (migration 2040000000177), journal_entry_lines debit/credit scale 3 (migration 0277), products.cost_price NUMERIC(20,6) (migration 0252) — no migration. EXCLUDED: variant-consolidation legs (two total_cost legs from different unit costs — valuation decision pending, still toFixed(2)) and comboOriginalPrice display shaping (RETURN-only); products.cost_price and stock_movements.unit_cost stay as supplied, only the total is rounded. Prospective only: already-posted opening stock and movements keep their stored amounts; no history is re-rounded.
- INV-PUBLIC-TRIAL-SIGNUP-OWNER-NAME (2 November 2026): POST /api/public/trial-signup accepts an optional ownerName (2–80 chars, validated by PublicTrialSignupDto, Arabic field label in the validation pipe); the signup service resolves it with the business name as fallback, so older clients that send no ownerName keep working byte-for-byte, and passes the resolved name to TrialTenantProvisioningService.createTrialTenant (stored as the tenant owner_name, the owner user's display_name and the ownerName setting) and to TrialSignupMailService, which greets the person by name in the Arabic/English text and HTML bodies (business name stays the fallback greeting). Deliberately unchanged: the duplicate check (email/phone), rate limits, honeypot, the response shape, and the website form validation (the form already required the name and now sends it — separate website patch, applied only after this backend package deploys, because the global pipe forbids non-whitelisted fields). No migration: no new column; the name flows into existing owner_name/display_name storage.
- INV-PRICING-EXTRA-BRANCH-CAP (2 November 2026): the extra-branch addon monthly price in pricing/pricing-catalog.json is capped at half the band's L1 monthly price, rounded half-up to a whole currency unit (band1 EG 225 / SA 75, band2 EG 325 / SA 100, band3 EG 700 / SA 175, band4 EG 750 / SA 200; band5 EG 1200 / SA 329 was already under the cap and is unchanged). The rule is enforced by validator rule 11 in pricing/validate-catalog.mjs (no addon above the cap, no addon price for a blocked country) and pinned by pricing-branch-addon-cap.spec.ts driving the real PricingCatalogService over the regenerated catalog. The values are served through the single public pricing endpoint (GET /api/public/pricing-catalog) like every other price, so the website and the in-app billing screen see the same numbers with no separate copy. Deliberately unchanged: level prices, extra-user addons, floors, and Gulf addon prices (Gulf stays null — contact-for-price, never invented numbers, as recorded in the strict-offer invariant); the old near-100%-of-L1 addon curve is replaced prospectively only, with no repricing of already-sold subscriptions.
 - INV-PRICING-REPRICE-V2 (2 November 2026): market-study reprice of the public list prices in pricing/pricing-catalog.json (single source, served through GET /api/public/pricing-catalog): Saudi restaurant (band3) 349/749/1499 -> 249/649/1299 and wholesale (band4) L1 399 -> 349, anchored on the published Saudi cards (Matbex 199, Noqqat/Saheeh 249); Gulf rows recomputed from the Saudi list at the catalog FX rule (SA USD x1.05, catalog FX 2026-09-25) with annuals staying monthly x 10 (validator rule 4) — 54 cells cut, ZERO raised: Bahrain/Oman cells the formula would have lifted (retail/wholesale/construction, e.g. BH wholesale L2 89 -> 95) were deliberately HELD at their old values and are pinned by pricing-reprice-v2.spec.ts, because no market evidence supports a rise. Egypt list prices UNCHANGED (900/2250/4500 etc., validated 20% below Daftra's annual card; narrower 300-500 EGP locals have unverified ETA depth). Extra-branch addons follow the new L1s under the cap (SA band3 125, band4 175; validator rule 11); extra-user addons unchanged; Gulf addons stay absent (contact-for-price); EG perpetual prices unchanged and SA/Gulf perpetual still follows offline.perpetualMultiplierFromAnnual (2.5x annual) wherever it is consumed (no backend consumer of the multiplier was found — display path unverified). Launch offer is catalog DATA, not a code constant: launchOffer percentOffFirstYear 20, firstYearOnly, newCustomersOnly, pinned by validator rule 12 (integer 1-50, both flags true) and surfaced as launchOffer on PublicPricing by PricingCatalogService on both the strict public and tenant-fallback paths — NO end date or customer cap is set (the owner names the limit later in a one-line follow-up; until then the offer reads open-ended for new customers' first year). Catalog version/updatedAt left at 1.0/2026-09-25 per the established pattern of the strict-offer and addon-cap packages. Prospective only: already-sold subscriptions keep their sold prices; no history is repriced. Website display of the launch offer is a separate website patch, after the backend deploys.
- INV-SALES-WRITE-RESIDUAL-BOOK-DIGITS (2 November 2026): every derived money residual in the sales writer rounds ONCE at the tenant book digits through sales-write.helper.ts (calculateStoreCreditBalance for customers.store_credit_balance on create/edit/cancel, calculateRemainingDebt for the edit/cancel ledger-reversal debt, roundSaleResidual for loyalty/discount intermediates and the cancel cash-refund treasury amount; cancelSale resolves moneyDigits on its own transaction handle, as create/update already did). Storage already holds 3dp for every target (migration 0258: sales/sale_payments/customers/customer_ledger; migration 0256: treasury_transactions.amount), so no migration rides with this package. Deliberately unchanged: loyalty-points balances (points, not money, stay at 2dp), the treasury reversal loop using RAW stored payment amounts (an exact reversal must not be re-rounded), held_sales.paid_amount (draft snapshot, NUMERIC(14,2) storage, no backend reader copies it into a posted sale), and computeInvoiceTotals (3dp ceiling shared with purchases, never truncates below book digits).
- INV-PDC-SUBLEDGER-BALANCE-AFTER (2 November 2026): PDC cheque actions record the partner running balance EXACTLY in customer_ledger/supplier_ledger.balance_after (Number(updated.balance), no re-rounding) in syncCustomerSubLedger/syncSupplierSubLedger, following the syncPostedPartnerLedger precedent; the partner balance is a SQL increment of book-digit amounts, so any rounding of balance_after diverges it from the stored balance by up to 0.005 on 3-decimal tenants. Storage already holds 3dp (migrations 0257/0258), so no migration rides with this package. Deliberately unchanged: the private toMoney(2dp) helper (return shaping for list/detail/stats responses only, never a stored balance), cheque face/book amounts and journal legs (owned by the PDC posting package), and the WHT subledger sync (separate package, same pattern).
- INV-BALANCE-REPAIR-BOOK-DIGITS (2 November 2026): the admin balance-repair actions (reconcileCustomers/reconcileSuppliers/reconcileAll) reconcile partner balances at tenant book digits: the ledger sum is rounded ONCE through minor units (fromMinorUnits/toMinorUnits at currencyMoneyDigits of the tenant base currency, resolved on the caller transaction handle, failing closed on currency-settings mismatch), the stored balance is compared EXACTLY with no skip band and no second rounding, a partner with no ledger rows and a non-zero stored balance is fail-closed (skipped and reported in the new skipped/skipped counts, never zeroed), and each run -- reconcileAll across both sides included -- writes inside ONE transaction with audit logging only after commit. Storage already holds 3dp (migrations 0257/0258: suppliers/customers balance), so no migration rides with this package. Deliberately unchanged: the settings diagnostics helper (toFixed(2) return shaping only), the destructive-admin gate (super_admin or canManageBackups), and the PDC/WHT subledger syncs (separate packages, same exact-balance pattern).
- INV-NO-FORCED-UPDATE-RELOAD (2 November 2026): a deployed build must never reload or take over a live session on its own. The service worker in frontend/src/main.tsx is registered for offline operation only: no skipWaiting on a waiting worker, and no update check on tab focus or visibility change. A new build applies on the next fresh navigation or on a user-initiated reload (including a hard refresh), never automatically in front of a cashier, a form, or a storefront popup. A lazy chunk that fails to load (for example a hashed chunk replaced by a new deploy) is retried once by re-importing and then shows the manual error fallback; the fallback's reload button is the only automatic-path reload, and it is user-initiated. Deliberately unchanged: data polling and React Query refetching (live orders, POS tables, catalog heartbeat) refresh data only and never reload the page, and persisted state (storefront cart in localStorage, POS workspace drafts) still survives a user-initiated reload while in-memory state (open popups, search text, scroll position) does not. Enforced by frontend/src/app/no-forced-reload.spec.ts. Prospective only: no stored data is changed.
- INV-REVERSAL-LINK-BOOK-DIGITS (2 November 2026): a PDC reversal link is recorded only when the candidate journal's lines offset the new journal's lines exactly at the tenant's book digits. `areOppositeJournalLines` takes the tenant's `MoneyDigits` as a required argument (never a silent cents default) and nets every financial dimension — account, partner, branch, location, cost center — in minor units; the caller `exactPdcReversalSource` receives the digits its two PDC callers already resolved on the caller transaction handle (`currencyMoneyDigits` over `tenantBaseCurrency`), with no new settings read. On a 3-decimal tenant 0.011 vs 0.009 must not link (2 fils apart) and 0.014 vs 0.006 + 0.008 must link (exact split); the old cents netting answered both backwards. A non-finite leg answers false (no link) instead of throwing, and an empty side never links. Prospective only: already-recorded reversal links are not rewritten.
- INV-SALE-EDIT-PAYMENT-GUARD-BOOK-DIGITS (2 November 2026): the sale-edit guard that refuses an edit when the header paid_amount exceeds the recorded sale_payments rows compares at the tenant's book digits. The pure helper `paidAmountExceedsRecordedPayments` takes the tenant's `MoneyDigits` as a required argument (never a silent cents default), rounds each leg once to minor units, keeps the strict `paid > recorded` semantics, and blocks the edit (fail closed) when any leg is non-finite. The caller in `updateSale` passes the `moneyDigits` it already resolved on the caller transaction handle; the second guard branch (no payment rows + mixed channel) is unchanged. On a 3-decimal tenant two 0.004 payments plus a 0.008 header must not block the edit, while a 0.009 header over the same rows must. The create-path cent validation inside `resolveSalePayments` is a separate payload contract and is deliberately untouched by this package. Prospective only: no posted journal or stored payment is rewritten.
- INV-VARIANT-CONSOLIDATION-BOOK-DIGITS (2 November 2026): variant-consolidation stock movement totals in `saveFashionGroup` use the single computation `stockMovementTotal(qty, unitCost, bookDigits)` with digits resolved once on the caller transaction handle (`bookDigits(trx, actor)`), like every other catalog total_cost site. This supersedes the consolidation exclusion in INV-CATALOG-STOCK-TOTALS-BOOK-DIGITS for ROUNDING ONLY: each leg keeps its own unit cost (target variant's costPrice for the inbound leg, removed product's cost_price for the outbound leg), and the valuation question — whether the two legs should share one unit cost — stays open and undecided. On a 3-decimal tenant 3 x 33.375 stores 100.125, not 100.13. Prospective only: already-stored consolidation movements keep their stored totals; no history is re-rounded. Digits follow the tenant's settings currency (KWD = 3, EGP = 2) through `tenantBaseCurrency`, fail closed on a settings/rate mismatch — the same gating as every other book-digits site.
- INV-COGS-REMAINDER-ABSORPTION (2 November 2026): sale COGS is the remainder-absorbed residual, not a once-rounded total. `cogsResidualAmount` (accounting/engines/cogs-residual.engine.ts) computes, per product and frozen unit cost, pre-sale stock value minus remaining stock value — both rounded at book digits, differenced in minor units — and `postSale` and `postSaleEdit` both post through the shared `saleCogsResidual` read, which sums the remaining quantity across all branches/locations (weighted cost is product-level) AFTER the sale's stock movement, on the same transaction handle. The diagnosed case is pinned: 16 units at 15.3125, selling 10, posts COGS 153.12 (not 153.13) so remaining GL 91.88 equals physical 6 x 15.3125; selling out keeps the old total rounding exactly. Digits follow the tenant's settings currency (KWD = 3, EGP = 2). Deliberately unchanged: the sales-return COGS reversal (`calculateSalesReturnCost`) and the field-return approval posting — their residual mirror needs the acceptance run to prove the revaluation interplay and is a named follow-up, not part of this package. Prospective only: already-posted sale journals keep their COGS legs; no history is re-rounded. This closes the deferred 0.01 divergence (D1) for sale postings.
- INV-SELLING-PRICE-SCALE-3 (2 November 2026): the remaining selling-price columns store three minor units. CORRECTION, recorded loudly: the Slice 2 grounding first claimed selling-price storage was NUMERIC(14,2) everywhere with no widening — that reading missed the loop-based widening migrations (0256-0259, 0260, 0263-0266, 0271, 0276, 0277), which list columns as data. The main chain was ALREADY widened (0258: sales totals, sale_items unit_price/line_total, sale_payments, customers, customer_ledger, products retail/wholesale; 0259: returns; 0277: journal lines; 0257: purchases; 0252: cost_price at 6dp; 0059: sales_orders at scale 3 from creation). Migration 2040000000278 (highest + 1, auto-discovered by FileMigrationProvider, no registration list) widens ONLY the remainder, capacity-preserving, skip when already scale >= 3, down a no-op: products credit_price/consumer_price/min_selling_price, sale_items consumer_price/unit_offer_savings, sales_order_items consumer_price/unit_offer_savings, held_sales paid/cash/card/discount/paid_online_amount, held_sale_items unit_price, online_orders subtotal/delivery_fee/total_amount; optional widen-if-present: online_orders discount_amount, held_sales delivery_fee, price_list_items fixed_price, product_offers value, product_customer_prices price. No code change: computations already round at book digits. Per-tenant behavior follows the settings currency (KWD = 3, EGP = 2); 2-decimal tenants' stored values are unchanged. Prospective only: already-stored prices keep their values. The migration must still run on the production DB via the migration runner; storage is not closed on a real DB until that run is confirmed.
- INV-PURCHASE-CANCEL-WAC-RECOMPUTE (2 November 2026): cancelling a received purchase re-blends the remaining pool at the ORIGINAL receipt value in the same transaction. `cancelPurchase` calls the canonical writer `revalueStockReturn` right after `applyStockDelta`, with the corrected `globalAfter` and the full removed base quantity, removing `roundMoney(removedQty x purchaseReceiptBaseUnitCost(stored unit_cost, unit_multiplier, header tax_rate, prices_include_tax), book digits)` — the exact inverse of the creation/receipt blend input, digits resolved once on the transaction handle (`currencyMoneyDigits` over `tenantBaseCurrency`), fail closed. The location-scoped availability guard is unchanged and makes cancel all-or-nothing per line, so a partial sale or transfer from the receiving location still blocks the cancel (adjustment documents required); valuation itself is product-global, like the purchase-return revaluation. A missing or corrupt stored unit cost fails the cancel closed (PURCHASE_CANCEL_COST_INVALID) instead of silently keeping the stale WAC. Deliberately unchanged: the journal reversal (exact mirror via `reversePurchaseJournal`), the supplier-balance and treasury reversals, the returns/landed-cost/paid guards, and the unreachable edit path (`updatePurchase` still blends nothing on reversal). On a 2-decimal tenant 30 units at 17.00 minus a 10-unit receipt at 11.00 re-blends to 20.00; on a 3-decimal tenant a tax-inclusive carton receipt (5 x 33.000 at 10% VAT, multiplier 2) removed from 20 units at 12.500 re-blends to 10.000. Prospective only: already-cancelled purchases keep their stored cost_price; no history is re-blended.
 - INV-PO-RECEIVED-QUANTITY-ADVANCE (2 November 2026): a physical receipt advances its purchase order in the same transaction as the stock write. `advancePurchaseOrderReceipt` (purchases/engines/purchase-order-receipt.engine.ts) is the single computation both receipt paths call — the GRN posting (`postGoodsReceipt`, accepted quantities only, grouped by purchase-order item) and the legacy receipt (`receivePurchaseGoods`, purchase-item units, never base stock units) — on the same transaction handle, after the stock writes, so a failed receipt advances nothing and the advance can never run outside a receipt. It locks the order and its items, adds the received quantity to each item's `received_quantity` at 3dp (NUMERIC(14,3) from creation, no migration), and moves the order status to `partially_received` when any item is short of its ordered quantity and to `received` only when every item is at or past it (0.001 tolerance). A `converted_to_bill` order keeps its status while its items still advance, so the conversion marker that blocks a second conversion survives. Receivable statuses are `confirmed`, `partially_received`, `received`, and `converted_to_bill` only; any other status, an item that does not belong to the order, or a bill item linked without its bill fails closed. Deliberately unchanged: the GRN-bill path (`createBillFromReceipts`) and conversion (`convertToBill`) post no stock and advance nothing — the stock was already advanced by the receipt — and the edit path (`updatePurchase`) is unreachable for every purchase status, so it advances nothing either. Prospective only: historical purchase orders keep their stored `received_quantity` of 0; no backfill, no journal rewrite.
 - INV-REORDER-EVALUATION-PIPELINE (2 November 2026): reorder evaluation counts only committed pipeline stock as incoming — `pending_approval`, `confirmed`, and `partially_received` at quantity minus received_quantity (trusted now that the Slice 3 P5 advance writes received_quantity on receipt) — and never counts a draft: a draft is the only status a generated order carries until a human confirms it (confirmOrder is the only draft-to-pipeline transition, drafts are editable and deletable, and last_generated_po_id has no reader), so counting drafts would treat uncommitted intent as stock, while excluding drafts without a quench would regenerate a draft on every run. The quench is a live draft purchase order holding the same product at the same warehouse (a warehouse-null draft quenches any warehouse for that product); while it is open no new order is generated for the rule, and cancelling or deleting it lifts the suppression, after which confirmation moves the order into the committed statuses and its remainder counts as incoming. A manual_review rule is evaluated and reported but generates nothing. Each evaluation for one tenant runs in a single transaction under the per-tenant reorder-evaluation advisory lock (pg_advisory_xact_lock over hashtext, the storefront/hr pattern) plus FOR UPDATE locks on the tenant active rule rows in id order, so the scheduler and manual runs serialise; generated order numbers derive from the inserted row id via formatDailyDocumentNumber, never from a row count. Deliberately unchanged: PO line totals stay with the reorder grouping engine, order-number days stay host-local as in createOrder, and historical orders and rules keep their numbers and statuses. Prospective only: no backfill, no journal rewrite.
- INV-IMPORT-LATE-EXPENSE-CONTRACT (2 November 2026): the late-expense dialog posts exactly the backend contract: singular route POST shipments/:shipmentId/late-expense with only ProcessLateExpenseDto fields (lateExpenseAmount, title, note, and accountId/payableAccountId only when the caller supplies them). The dialog never sends expenseCategory, handlingMethod, treasuryAccountId or idempotencyKey; the treasury account is never mapped to accountId because the backend reads accountId as a COGS-account override and the endpoint posts a payable, never a treasury movement. The success summary is read only from allocation.lateCogsAdjustmentEgp and allocation.lateInventoryAdjustmentEgp. Allocation is always proportional sold-vs-on-hand; the dialog states that plainly and offers no charge-all-to-COGS treatment. Prospective only: no posted journal is rewritten.
- INV-IMPORT-APPLY-PRICES-NO-WAC-OVERWRITE (2 November 2026): applySuggestedPrices never writes products.cost_price (the blended WAC) and never overwrites supplier_id or category_id for any product; only retail_price and wholesale_price move, from the existing pricingCostEgp computation (landed cost adjusted for a pricing exchange rate different from the arrival rate). Product rows are locked FOR UPDATE in global ID order, grouped by account scope through the canonical lockStockProducts helper, inside the same transaction as the price writes. An unknown shipment id fails closed with NotFound instead of silently succeeding. Prospective only: already-applied prices keep their values; no WAC is re-blended.
- INV-IMPORT-PO-FROM-PROFORMA-FAIL-CLOSED (2 November 2026): generatePoFromProforma never borrows items from any other project: a proforma without usable items fails closed at the empty-items guard, and the old auto-recovery block (same-project feasibility, then the tenant's most recently created project) is removed — its mapping dropped productId, so recovered lines always failed validation and the transaction rolled back, and it changed no working behaviour. A lines foreign total differing from the proforma header total by more than 0.02 throws IMPORT_PROFORMA_TOTAL_MISMATCH and leaves the header total untouched; the 0.02 tolerance itself is unchanged. Prospective only: already-issued POs keep their lines and totals.
- INV-IMPORT-RECEIPT-SUPPLIER-FAIL-CLOSED: import receipt and late-expense journals credit only the shipment supplier, else the linked project's feasibility supplier, resolved and validated against the tenant's suppliers BEFORE any stock or journal write; no fallback to the tenant's first supplier by id and no auto-created generic supplier (the generic-supplier creation path was removed from both flows). Every receipt journal line and header carries the receipt location and its branch; late-expense lines keep null branch/location because on-hand is read company-wide across locations and no single receipt location can be attributed honestly. Failure is a named BadRequestException ([IMPORT_RECEIPT_SUPPLIER_REQUIRED]) with the transaction rolled back.
- INV-IMPORT-PROJECT-PROFORMA-GUARDS: project status never moves through the plain PATCH (status changes only through closeProject with its maker-checker approval and settlement checks; a PATCH-supplied status is rejected with [IMPORT_PROJECT_STATUS_VIA_CLOSE_ONLY]) and feasibilityData is frozen once the project is closed. A proforma freezes fully once its PO is issued; after any payment only an items-only update is allowed (the established workflow pays before linking items to catalog products, and PO generation re-validates the header total against the items) while all money/identity fields are rejected. Approval requires draft status and at least one validated item (quantity/qty > 0 or a non-empty name; feasibility items without a catalog productId are valid). Creation requires the supplier to exist in the tenant and a per-tenant unique pi_number via a service check only (no unique index: concurrent creates can still race). RESIDUAL: maker-checker approver != creator cannot be enforced — import_proforma_invoices has no created_by column; enforcing it needs a migration.
- INV-IMPORT-TRANSFER-APPROVAL-BINDING: Large-transfer approvals are bound: verifyAndConsumeApprovalRequest enforces supplier, proforma invoice, project, currency and amount ceiling from expectedDetails; createApprovalRequest validates large_transfer payloads (positive amount ceiling, 3-letter currency, supplier/reference match); the >=100,000 threshold is normalised to the tenant base currency via resolveFxRate; the contract rate defaults to pi.exchange_rate_contract, never to cleanEgp/cleanForeign; unlinked transfers without a caller or PI rate fail closed with IMPORT_EXCHANGE_RATE_REQUIRED.
- INV-IMPORT-PROJECT-NUMBERING: Project numbers are minted by the advisory-locked daily sequence generator (generateDailySequenceNumber) on the tenant-local business day, never COUNT(*) + 1; an explicitly supplied projectNo is kept and guarded by the unique index on (tenant_id, project_no) added by migration 0279.
- INV-IMPORT-LATE-EXPENSE-IDEMPOTENCY: Late expenses honour an optional idempotency key recorded as a provisional event row under the partial unique index from migration 0280 (duplicate keys conflict, keyless calls unchanged); allocation follows the shipment cost_allocation_method (value/weight/cbm) with the landed-cost engine's guards; per-item COGS/inventory adjustments round at book digits with the remainder on the last item so the parts sum exactly to the journal totals.
- INV-IMPORT-ARRIVAL-RATE-FAIL-CLOSED: No shipment item may be costed and no shipment may be marked Arrived without a real positive exchange_rate_at_arrival ([IMPORT_ARRIVAL_RATE_REQUIRED]); createShipment persists DTO-supplied rates; migration 0281 drops the DEFAULT 1 for new rows only, and legacy rows stored at exactly 1 remain indistinguishable from a genuinely entered 1.
- INV-IMPORT-SHIPMENT-CAPITAL-GUARDS: Received quantity may never exceed the B/L quantity (validateImportLine rule); the same container_number + bill_of_lading pair per tenant is rejected, with the pre-existing (tenant_id, container_number) unique index as backstop; capital movements honour an optional idempotency key under the partial unique index from migration 0282; per-project partner percentages are authoritative with a <= 100.001 ceiling enforced in createProject and setProjectPartners, and the global import_partners percentage is legacy and unenforced.
- INV-IMPORT-PAYMENT-METHODS: Every foreign transfer and broker payment carries a closed payment-method enum (bank_transfer | cash_carried | letter_of_credit | cash_against_documents | open_account) with method-specific required evidence validated before any write — bank transfer requires a payment reference, cash carried requires carrier name + travel date + handover receipt and an explicit custody account, LC requires number + issuing bank + expiry + document list, CAD requires documents-handover proof, open account requires explicit terms + due date; advance+balance percentages are explicit (no silent 30 default; one-sided or non-100 splits fail closed); the P5 approval binding carries paymentMethod in expectedDetails; legacy method-less writes are recorded as bank_transfer with no new demands, and existing records are never rewritten. (Migration 0283.)
- INV-IMPORT-FORM4-CLEARANCE-GATE: Form 4 number/date are optional when a transfer is recorded but REQUIRED before a shipment transitions to In Customs or Arrived when the shipment's B/L value exceeds USD 2,000 under the single named constant FORM4_REQUIRED_THRESHOLD_USD and any project-linked funding transfer is bank-transfer-funded (exempt only when recorded funding methods are non-empty and all cash_carried; no recorded funding means the gate does not apply); the gate fails closed with the named error [IMPORT_FORM4_REQUIRED] before landed-cost recalculation or receipt posting, and is satisfied by the shipment's own Form 4 fields or a project-linked transfer's form4_number. (Migration 0284.)
- INV-IMPORT-RECEIPT-REVERSAL: A posted import receipt is reversed only prospectively, only once per receipt (DB-unique reversal row), and only through an approval-gated maker-checker token bound to the shipment and receipt: the reversal restores stock via applyStockDelta in canonical lock order with allowNegative false, un-blends WAC via revalueStockReturn from stock_movements as source of truth, zeroes and reverses the lots, flips allocated preorders back to reserved, and posts a mirror journal linked via reversalOfEntryId that never rewrites the original journal (no journal when the original posted none); it fails closed with full rollback when any lot was consumed, a late-expense event exists, a preorder is invoiced, the project is closed, or the period is closed. A legacy_review_required receipt resolves only through an approval-gated review with posted-journal plus movement proof, setting posted_at from the earliest movement evidence, never silently and never with a fabricated date. No shipment/item deletion endpoints exist for this path. (Migration 0285.)
- INV-IMPORT-BROKER-CAPITALISATION: A liquidated broker expense capitalises exactly once and through exactly one path: pre-receipt it folds into the shipment cost bucket (floor-enforced so the bucket can never drop below the folded sum, one link row, no journal and no expenses row) and is consumed at receipt by the split journal Dr inventory / Cr supplier payable for landed-minus-folded / Cr broker-advance asset for the folded amount; post-receipt it flows through the shared late-expense revaluation core (WAC, lots and landed-cost fields in the same transaction, journal Dr inventory / Cr broker-advance, linked late-expense event); refundable VAT posts to the VAT asset and non-capitalised expenses to COGS with no link row; capitalised expenses with no shipment, a legacy frozen receipt, or no bucket (other / non-refundable VAT) fail closed before any write; no expense is ever self-verified — verification is maker-checker via an approval token bound to the expense, the liquidator recorded as created_by can never verify their own expense, and unverified broker expenses block project closure; a cost already counted in shipment.customs_cost_egp can never capitalise twice. (Migration 0286.)
- INV-IMPORT-FX-TRIPS-POSTING: An FX acquisition is a posted ledger event, never a memo row: recordFxAcquisition inserts the acquisition (currency normalised to uppercase at write), posts one balanced journal Dr destination / Cr source for the EGP cost via postDomainJournal, and writes two treasury legs (+destination / -source) in the same transaction, linked by the acquisition serial_id; an optional transfer link is attribution only and requires the transfer to belong to the tenant with a case-insensitively matching currency. A sourcing trip's actual_cost_egp becomes a project expense only through approval-gated postSourcingTripExpense (token bound to trip, amount ceiling and supplier; expense-type account; one journal Dr expense / Cr supplier payable mirrored to the supplier subledger; one expenses row; second posting conflicts), and the project profit report counts only trips with journal_entry_id set, so unposted estimates are memo-only and excluded from financial reports and a posted trip can never double-count. (Migration 0287.)
- INV-IMPORT-PROFIT-HONESTY: Project profit reads ONLY posted ledger evidence: the shortage expense from posted receipt journals (netted with their Batch-3 reversal mirrors in the same period), non-capitalised broker expenses from posted import_broker_expense journals counted exactly once (folded and revalued broker costs are never re-counted — they already sit inside the receipt / late-expense journals), signed realised FX variance from proforma-linked supplier_payment journals (a gain reduces expenses; unlinked transfers are excluded), and deposit deductions from posted container-refund journals; posted sourcing trips (journal_entry_id set) and posted late-expense COGS portions count, while unposted trip estimates and unposted late-expense memos are reported as excluded and never enter profit (profitBasis POSTED_LEDGER_ONLY for project reports; the company-wide branch keeps its expenses-table basis and is labelled as such); component sums run over the resolver-eligible account sets (settings override else candidate COA codes, active and correctly typed) so a missing role account yields zero instead of breaking the read, while posting paths still fail closed; the container autopsy reports realised gross trading margin — never net profit — labels the exchange-rate source (arrival / pricing / assumed_50), states factory cost on both bill-of-lading and received quantities, states the frozen landed value only from a posted receipt journal, and breaks broker expenses down by the path each one took.
- INV-IMPORT-TARIFF-VERSIONING: Customs tariffs are explicit versioned data: no read, list, calculator or background path ever seeds or overwrites tariff rows — the only refresh is the explicit POST customs-tariffs/sync-official manual path, which is insert-only (a tenant-edited row is never overwritten) and stamps every row it inserts with TARIFF_DATA_VERSION (decree-218-2022), returning inserted/skipped/total counts as the refresh log; reads return exactly the last explicit version (possibly empty); the calculator labels an unknown HS code (zeros plus isDocumentedTariff false plus a warning message, surfaced as a warning in the UI) instead of silently using a default rate. (Migration 0288.)
- INV-IMPORT-SECTOR-PRESET: Sector is a per-project data choice, never a hardcoded branch: import_projects.sector_preset holds the preset key (NULL means generic); unknown keys fail closed at creation, update and import with [IMPORT_UNKNOWN_SECTOR_PRESET], and the preset locks once shipment items exist for the project ([IMPORT_SECTOR_PRESET_LOCKED]); the truck_parts preset (first preset) keeps the sector conventions verbatim — truck category, OEM map, fabricated SKU/barcode, metadata defaults and 1.35/1.20 markups on NEW products only; the generic path creates no category, invents no SKU, barcode, OEM number or metadata default, and prices new products at estimated cost with no markup; importing feasibility items never rewrites an existing product matched by name under any preset — it links as-is; the import fails closed with [IMPORT_EXCHANGE_RATE_REQUIRED] when neither an arrival rate nor a feasibility commercial rate exists (no silent 50). (Migration 0289.)
- INV-IMPORT-DEAD-CODE-REMOVAL: calculateImportLandedCosts was removed after a whole-repo call-site grep at the Batch 4 base proved zero callers (only its definition and one import line existed); the live landed-cost path runs through groupImportReceipts and the service calculation, and validateImportLine, requireImportNumber and importWeightedAverageCost stay because they have live callers; the frontend mutations useCollectPreorderDepositMutation and useRefundPreorderDepositMutation were removed after a whole-frontend grep proved zero .tsx callers and the backend has no /preorders/:id/deposit or /preorders/:id/refund-deposit routes (the live preorder refund is POST /preorders/:id/refund); the 'clearance_fees' default in the live liquidate mutation is a pre-existing latent, not dead code, and is left untouched. No behaviour change; no migration.
- INV-IMPORT-QUOTATION-LOCK: Selecting a quotation is a locked, single-winner decision: the candidate row is locked FOR UPDATE, only a pending quotation can be selected ([IMPORT_QUOTATION_NOT_SELECTABLE] otherwise), an expired quotation can never be selected ([IMPORT_QUOTATION_EXPIRED]; only strictly-past expiry dates expire), re-selecting the same selected quotation is an idempotent no-op that changes nothing, and the whole (project, item) sibling group is locked before deciding so concurrent selections serialise and exactly one winner emerges; a quotation created at or before an existing selection for the same (project, item) belongs to the locked round and cannot supersede it ([IMPORT_QUOTATION_SELECTION_LOCKED]) — only a quotation created after the selection (a new round) may supersede, rejecting the previous winner in the same transaction; a failed selection mutates nothing; there is no edit or delete path for quotations — a new quotation is the only path to change; prospective only. No migration.
INV-IMPORT-PREARRIVAL-CONVERSION-GUARD: A container preorder converts to a sale only after a POSTED inventory receipt exists for its shipment and the shipment's active lots cover the preorder quantity: otherwise convertPreorderToSale fails closed with IMPORT_PREORDER_CONVERSION_NO_POSTED_RECEIPT or IMPORT_PREORDER_CONVERSION_NOT_ALLOCATED before any write (no sale, no journal, no stock movement). The guard checks the receipt and the lots directly, never the preorder status (a preorder created after posting stays reserved). The stock deduction runs with allowNegative: false and the stock movement stamps the true scope before/after quantities. Prospective only; the branch/location default, hardcoded zero tax and sales-write bypass in the conversion path are pre-existing and unchanged.
INV-IMPORT-SUPPLIER-ATTRIBUTION: Every import cost component carries an explicit payee resolved before posting: a posted receipt splits its credit into one payable/clearing line per nonzero component (goods, freight, customs, transport, demurrage) attributed to that component's explicit supplier, and the lines sum to exactly the old single-line total; a late expense credits its named payee or, with a recorded reason, the tenant-designated clearing account — never silently the goods supplier. A nonzero component with no payee and no recorded unallocable reason fails closed with IMPORT_COMPONENT_PAYEE_REQUIRED before any write; a late expense with neither fails with IMPORT_LATE_EXPENSE_PAYEE_REQUIRED. Clearing is only for fees proven genuinely unallocable: the reason is recorded on the shipment/event, the line is labelled with it, and the clearing account is designated explicitly in accounting_settings (clearing_account_id, migration 0290) with no COA-code fallback and never a flagged control account (1130/2110). Prospective only; journals posted before migration 0290 keep their single-payee lines.
INV-IMPORT-POST-POSTING-COSTS-LEAVE: A cost difference landing after an import receipt is posted never silently reprices posted stock: the approved IAS 2 revaluation (late expenses / broker capitalisation) is the only path that moves posted costs, and it moves them openly — sold share to COGS, on-hand share to inventory/WAC/lots, always with its balanced journal — while every recalculation/edit entry (calculateLandedCost, cost edits, item edits, adding items) fails closed once any receipt row exists, and a closed accounting period blocks every posting through the posting boundary. No 5190 variance path exists or is introduced: routing late costs to variance would misstate both inventory and COGS. The receipt reversal mirror is the only other posted-cost writer and it restores (never reprices). Prospective only; closed periods untouched. No production-code change; the LEAVE is pinned by spec only.
INV-IMPORT-ESTIMATES-HONEST: No silent estimate default in any import money path: money-creating paths fail closed with a named error when a required input is missing (suggested prices reject IMPORT_ARRIVAL_RATE_REQUIRED when a pricing rate is recorded with no arrival rate; the FX-acquisition payload builder rejects IMPORT_FX_RATE_REQUIRED when neither an EGP cost nor a rate is supplied, instead of inventing a rate of 50), and every estimate output carries its basis — quotations record exchange_rate_used plus assumed flags (migration 0291), the feasibility cash-flow schedule states the actual advance/balance percentages in its milestone text with is_estimate / assumed-split / rate fields, and the demurrage-risk result carries an assumptions object naming every defaulted input (14 free days, $50/day, rate 1) with its effective values. Display-only prefill defaults that the operator sees and can change (proforma contract-rate input, supplier-credit autofill, project rate displays) are kept and documented, never silent postings.
 - INV-COGS-REMAINDER-SOLD-UNIT-BASIS (2 November 2026): the COGS remainder-absorption residual is computed on the sold-unit basis — sale_items.cost_price is per sold unit (frozen WAC x unit_multiplier plus restaurant modifier costs) while product_location_stock.qty is in base units, so saleCogsResidual converts the remaining quantity to sold units (remaining VALUE at the frozen per-base-unit costs, divided by the per-sold-unit frozen cost) before cogsResidualAmount; with no modifiers this is remaining / unit_multiplier. Restaurant modifier components are anchored at most once per sale at their own frozen WAC (costPrice / consumptionQty from the stored modifiers JSON, plain array or { mods, offer } wrapper), never re-read at current WAC, and a component that is itself a sold line is anchored by its own line group only. Each product-and-cost group anchors the full remaining quantity, as before — so a product sold at two frozen costs in one sale carries one minor unit of anchor rounding per group, and the remaining GL equals physical exactly for the single-group-per-product cases. This clarifies INV-COGS-REMAINDER-ABSORPTION for multi-unit and modifier sales; the sales-return residual mirror is a separate deferred follow-up and must not assume the old base-unit basis. Prospective only.
 - INV-SALE-TAX-BOOK-DIGITS-PRE-ROUND (3 November 2026): the sale writer rounds sale-side tax/total to the tenant's book digits at the `roundSaleInvoiceTotals` seam before invoice allocation, at both the create and the edit call sites, because `computeInvoiceTotals` always rounds to 3 decimals while `allocateInvoiceDiscount` fails closed on anything finer than book digits — so a 2-decimal tenant's sale whose tax needs a third decimal (subtotal 100.00, discount 33.33, 14% VAT, tax 9.334) was rejected outright before any write. The shared totals util keeps its 3dp ceiling (purchases rely on it) and the allocation engine keeps its fail-closed contract; postSale re-rounds these same book-digits values, so the stored tax_amount/total are exactly the amounts journaled and the journal legs are unchanged. Prospective only.
 - INV-CONVERSION-LINE-DISCOUNTS-PRESERVED (3 November 2026): per-line discounts survive order and quotation conversion exactly: convertToSale in sales-orders.service.ts and quotations.service.ts carries each stored line discount as a per-line TOTAL discount (lineDiscount) into the sale payload, buildPreparedSaleItem subtracts it from that line's gross total before the header discount is allocated over the lines, and the per-line discounts count toward the discount gate in createSale and updateSale — POS payloads never send lineDiscount, so direct sales are unchanged, and the legacy per-item discount field is deliberately not read, so storefront and van-pre-sales conversions keep today's dropped-field behaviour. Before any sale is created, resolveConversionDiscounts reconciles the stored discount rows against each other and fails closed with CONVERSION_DISCOUNT_MISMATCH: each line discount is finite, non-negative and no more than its line gross total, and the stored header discount_amount equals the line discounts plus a non-negative global discount, which becomes the sale payload's header discount; the stored per-line total is deliberately not reconciled (creation stores it verbatim with no runtime validation and a 0 default, and conversion never reads it — the sale is recomputed from quantity, unit_price and discount alone), and the stored total_amount is client-supplied verbatim at order/quotation creation and is never trusted as the reconciliation target. Payment terms (cash forcing) and conversion lifecycle/claims/idempotency are separate packages and are untouched. Prospective only.
 - INV-OFFER-SELECTION-SHARED-POS-VAN (3 November 2026): POS and van field sales share one offer-selection rule — pickBestApplicableOffer in sales-write.helper.ts: lowest adjusted unit price among offers active at the sale instant, qualified on the SOLD quantity, with start/end dates and happy-hour/days-of-week windows evaluated from one captured instant in host-local parts (the POS reference clock) on both channels; the van per-type price derivation, fraud gates, 5-second fingerprint dedup and 4dp baseQty stock quantity are unchanged; the BOGO bonus gate requires a BOGO active under the same shared rule; van pre-sales order pricing and the van catalog display still select offers with their own rules and are named follow-ups; tenant-local offer clocks remain an open decision (P9) and the tenant-timezone business-date mechanism is not used for offer windows.
INV-CODEBASE-MAP: docs/CODEBASE_MAP.md is the static text codebase map grounded at HEAD faf0d28a788c67f4b76f4219bb07f68bfc855a68 (git ls-files counts at that HEAD, 4686 tracked files); regenerate it when a module or feature directory is added, removed, or renamed, or when an entry point moves, and never hand-edit its counts; docs/ENGINE_MAP.md remains the source of truth for money engines and ARCHITECTURE_INVARIANTS.md remains the source of truth for accounting rules, and the map asserts neither.
 - INV-FLOATING-POOL-ALLOCATION-LEG (3 November 2026): sale deduction allocates assigned locations first, then the unassigned (floating) pool as the final leg, capped at the pool row's qty minus its own reserved_qty (the same pooled-availability formula the availability check uses; reservations are booked on the location row by reserveLocationStock, never pooled). The pool leg is attributed to the first leg's location (or the sale location when no assigned leg exists) because sale_line_stock_allocations.location_id is NOT NULL and applyStockDelta's auto-drain physically re-homes the pool quantity into that location row before deducting, so the existing edit/cancel restore paths round-trip through the same row. When pooled availability is genuinely short the sale still fails closed with the true pooled available quantity in the INSUFFICIENT_STOCK error (previously 0). Applies to both the createSale and updateSale deduction loops and their restaurant-modifier sub-loops; overselling policy, reservation booking/release, FEFO batch gating, and the availability check itself are unchanged.
- INV-CONVERSION-PAYMENT-TERMS-CARRIED (3 November 2026): order/quotation conversion carries the stored payment terms into the sale payload instead of forcing cash: sales_orders.payment_terms ('cash' | 'credit', written only by van pre-sales; dashboard orders default to 'cash') resolves through resolveConversionPaymentTerms — missing/blank is cash, 'credit' produces the sale writer's own full-credit shape (paymentType/paymentChannel 'credit', no payment lines) so createSale's existing customer-required, credit-blocked and credit-limit checks consume it unchanged, and any other stored value throws CONVERSION_PAYMENT_TERMS_UNKNOWN before any sale is created (inside the claim try for sales orders, so 'converting' reverts to 'confirmed'). Quotations carry no terms column and always convert as cash. No partial/deposit/due-date shape exists on either document, so none is carried or invented. Lifecycle/claims/idempotency (P6c), per-line discounts (P6a), storefront conversion and direct POS payment handling are unchanged.
 - INV-VAN-DEDUP-DISTINGUISHING-KEY (3 November 2026): a completed van field sale is never returned from a fingerprint-keyed in-process cache — the basket fingerprint (tenant+trip+rep+customer+items signature) identifies a basket, not a submission, so two genuinely identical baskets sold seconds apart post as two distinct sales; completed-result dedup rests on the client-supplied clientTxId only (30s txKey cache backed by the durable [tx:clientTxId] database note check), concurrent duplicates still collapse through the in-flight promise map keyed on the fingerprint, and the 4-second database rapid-fire backstop (identical product/quantity multiset, INV-VAN-RAPID-FIRE-SIGNATURE) is unchanged; the field-collection twin path is a separate follow-up and is untouched.
- INV-CONVERSION-LIFECYCLE-RACES (3 November 2026): order/quotation conversion lifecycle is claim-guarded end to end — confirmAndReserve reads the order and its items inside one transaction under a FOR UPDATE row lock (no double reservation); both conversions claim an atomic 'converting' state (quotations: from any status except 'converted'/'converting', restoring the previous status on failure) and reserve a durable operation_executions row for the conversion idempotency key before createSale runs (the key was inert before: nothing reserved a row for it); a stuck 'converting' document recovers from that row's state — 'committed' re-attaches the existing sale, 'failed'/stale-'processing'/stale-claim reverts the claim and re-claims atomically (quotations: a stale claim found already-'converting' is taken over by refreshing its timestamp under a staleness guard, never by inventing a pre-claim status), fresh 'processing'/'recovery_required'/fresh-claim fails closed; a failed sale records its failure on the reserved row so the next retry proceeds at once; deleting a 'converting' order releases its reservation and deletes only when the same evidence proves no sale committed, re-checked under the row lock and again after the product locks. Cancel-order on a 'converting' order is unchanged (separate follow-up).
- INV-SALE-EDIT-COST-FLOOR (3 November 2026): the selling cost floor (max of frozen unit cost and min selling price, on the discount-allocated net line, with the allowed-price pre-approved override) is enforced by the shared findBelowFloorSaleItems helper at sale creation AND on sale edit; on edit the check runs after the frozen-cost restore so it uses the cost that will actually be stored and posted, a below-floor edit requires a floor approval via authorizeDiscountOverride and carries the floor-approval note, and an admin edit with no manager PIN is rejected.
- INV-SALES-RETURN-COGS-RESIDUAL-MIRROR (3 November 2026): a sales return reverses COGS on the same residual basis the sale posted it — the returned quantity per original sale line is fed through the shared residual grouping (saleCogsResidual's sold-unit conversion, per product-and-cost groups, modifier anchoring) with the post-sale remaining quantity reconstructed as the post-restock stock read minus the restocked base quantity, so GL inventory equals physical after sale + return in the worked case (stock 16 at 15.3125, sell 10, return 6: reversal 91.87, GL 183.75) and a full return nets COGS to zero; sequential returns telescope to the posted COGS. Prepared-composite lines (saved cost zero) contribute nothing and their COGS stays expensed; a return with no linked sale, an unresolvable line, or a differing nonzero saved cost keeps the legacy plain qty x frozen cost computation. Restock quantities, the WAC re-blend, revenue/tax legs, and refund tenders are unchanged. Prospective only.
- INV-SALE-EDIT-PREPARED-WASTE (3 November 2026): a served dine-in/takeaway/delivery sale is prepared waste under the same detection cancelSale uses — the shared detectPreparedWasteSale helper (consumed-ingredient allocation or active recipe meal, plus KDS cooking/ready/served or a non-pending stage on a sale older than 14h / with an unreadable date; the predicate itself is unchanged) — and editing such a sale treats its consumed allocations exactly as cancel does: restoration in updateSale is capped per product at the edited lines' new consumption (parent quantities plus modifier component quantities, mirroring the creation deduction formulas), the allocation-completeness throw is skipped for waste sales because the un-restored remainder is consumed food rather than a gap, and postSaleEdit excludes the consumed portion's residual COGS from the 1140/5100 reversal lines (old-lines residual minus restored-lines residual, both read against the same post-movement remaining stock, applied equally to the inventory debit and COGS credit sides and failing closed if it exceeds either side) while the re-post covers the new lines unchanged, so net COGS retains the waste cost and GL inventory equals physical stock; kept lines restore fully and exclude zero, removed lines restore nothing and exclude their full old residual. Retail sales (predicate false: no consumed-ingredient allocation and no recipe meal) restore and re-post exactly as before. Prospective only.
- INV-CROSS-SHIFT-CANCEL-CASH-ATTRIBUTION (3 November 2026): a cash refund on cancelSale is linked to the open shift that took the sale's cash — the sale creator's shift whose window (created_at to closed_at) contains the sale, branch-scoped like the fallback lookup and hasOpenCashierShift — so that shift nets the restored receipt against the refund and the cancelling shift is untouched; when the original shift is closed its stored expected cash is frozen and never restated, so the refund keeps the previous fallback (the cancelling user's latest open shift on the branch, or a sale-linked row for an admin with no open shift). Same-shift cancels and the admin no-shift exemption are unchanged. Prospective only.
- INV-SHIFT-SALE-CASH-ATTRIBUTION (3 November 2026): one attribution key attributes a sale's cash to a shift — the shared shiftSaleCashAttribution predicate used by the sales breakdown, the cancelled-sale receipt restore, and the attributed sale-cash totals/items: a sale belongs to its creator's shift when created inside that shift's window on the same branch/location, or, when no shift of its creator covers it, to the only open shift covering it on the branch/location (a sale posted by another login at the same terminal; ambiguous when two shifts are open, it belongs to none); rep-custody cash (collection_status prepaid_by_rep, including van field cash) never takes the fallback. The drawer movement view now shows the attributed sale cash rows (saleCashTotal plus sale items) under that same key; the expected-cash computation still reads the sales breakdown, so the display can never double-count the cash. Prospective only.
- INV-ADMIN-CASH-SALE-SHIFT-REQUIRED (3 November 2026): the open-shift gate on createSale applies to every cash receipt regardless of role — the admin/super_admin exemption for non-POS cash sales is removed, so an admin cash sale without an open shift on the branch fails with OPEN_SHIFT_REQUIRED, and with one it attributes exactly like a cashier sale under the shared shift attribution key; admins can hold shifts (openCashierShift exempts them from branch assignment), so the gate never deadlocks. Non-cash admin sales, credit sales, POS sales (already gated for all roles), and the cancelSale admin no-shift refund exemption are unchanged. Known behaviour change: merchant-initiated conversions that post cash through createSale (storefront convertToSale for uncollected/COD orders, van pre-sale conversion) now also require the converting user to hold an open shift. Prospective only.
- INV-CASH-DRAWER-MOVEMENT-TYPES (3 November 2026): drawer movements have four types — cash_in, cash_out, safe_drop, safe_replenishment. Safe drops and replenishments post asset-to-asset transfer journals (safe_drop: Dr counterpart / Cr drawer cash; safe_replenishment: Dr drawer cash / Cr counterpart) with source types cash_drawer_safe_drop / cash_drawer_safe_replenishment, never the 6950 expense or 7100 income legs, and no treasury_transfers row is written. There is no safe/cash-box account in the chart of accounts, so the transfer counterpart is caller-named (counterpartAccountId) and validated exactly like a treasury transfer — active is_cash_bank asset with a debit normal balance in the tenant, different from the drawer cash account — failing closed with DRAWER_COUNTERPART_REQUIRED / TREASURY_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND / DRAWER_COUNTERPART_SAME_AS_CASH; cash_in/cash_out ignore the counterpart. Unknown movement types fail closed with DRAWER_MOVEMENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN: normalizeCashDrawerMovementType validates (only an absent/empty type still defaults to cash_in) and postCashDrawerMovement throws instead of returning unposted. safe_drop takes the manager-approval path like cash_out, signs negative like cash_out, and flows into expected cash through the existing sign-based movement totals; movement items keep the cash_in/cash_out kind union with distinct safe labels. Idempotency is unchanged: the stored treasury row carries no counterpart, so a retry with the same key returns the first movement (same-key-same-operation). The frontend movement form still offers only cash_in/cash_out — the new types are API-available first. No denomination/count breakdown exists in the backend, so none is built here. Prospective only.
- INV-BLIND-CLOSE-DECLARED-RECONCILIATION (3 November 2026): a blind close's declared card/wallet/instapay totals are reconciled against the computed breakdowns at manager review, at book digits, and the reconciliation (declared, computed, delta, declared count, computed count, count delta per channel) is stored durably in the blind-close note payload and returned by the review call; the computed card side is card sales + service card − card return refunds, matching the review screen's definition, and a mismatch never blocks review or changes the cash variance posting. Declared totals and operation detail amounts are stored at book digits from the close (the legacy 2dp rounding truncated KWD declarations); values stored at 2dp before this package parse back identically and reconcile against their stored figures. The review dialog's client-side diff stays transient display only. Prospective only.
- INV-VAN-SETTLE-BOOK-DIGITS-VARIANCE (3 November 2026): settleTrip computes its cash variance at tenant book digits through the shared computeCashDrawerVariance helper — expected cash is roundMoney(grossCashCollected − tripExpenses, digits) and the variance is the helper's counted − expected — exactly like submitTripSettlement, so submit and settle agree on the same trip figures; the rep financial-ledger gates are nonzero-at-book-digits (any nonzero book variance records a cash_shortage/cash_overage row). The 0.5 suspicion gate is a currency materiality policy and is unchanged, as is the stock-shortage ledger gate on the engine-computed stock cost. This supersedes the deliberate non-convergence recorded under INV-CASH-VARIANCE-UNIFICATION. Prospective only.
- INV-CASHIER-SHORTAGE-TOLERANCE-DEFAULT (3 November 2026): when cashierShortageTolerance is unset (no settings row, null, or empty), the shift shortage tolerance defaults to one minor unit at book digits (the existing maxAllowedDifference): a dust shortage at or below one minor unit posts to the 7210 shortage expense, never to the 1135 employee receivable. An explicitly configured value — including 0 — is honoured exactly with no silent override; unparseable or negative values and read errors fail closed to 0. Shortages above the tolerance still charge the full amount (not just the excess) to 1135 on the custodian, exactly as before; overages are unchanged. There is no settings UI for this key. Prospective only.
