- INV-EXPECTED-CASH-SINGLE-WINDOW (3 November 2026): expected cash windows every time-windowed component on the row's created_at — the moment the cash moved — inside [shift.created_at, shift.closed_at]: sales, collections, returns, expenses, services, and supplier payments alike. Business dates (expense_date, service_date, payment_date) are stored and displayed on their own screens but never drive drawer attribution, so a movement counts exactly once across consecutive shifts of the same opener instead of twice (the old expense/service OR predicate) or in the wrong shift (the old payment_date-only supplier predicate). The shift-linked treasury legs carry no time predicate and are exactly-once by link; the movement list shown on the shift uses the same created_at rule. Prospective only.
- INV-REP-CASH-REMITTANCE-SHIFT (3 November 2026): when a van trip settles, the counted cash the rep hands over is linked to the receiving shift by exactly one treasury row (txn_type rep_cash_remittance, reference_type cashier_shift, deterministic request_key rep-remittance:trip:<id>) written in the same transaction as the settlement journal, and NO second GL journal is posted for the handover (the settlement journal already debits 1110 for the counted cash; postCashDrawerMovement must never run for it). The receiving shift is the settle actor's own open shift on the branch, else the branch's latest open shift; no open shift fails the whole settle closed with OPEN_SHIFT_REQUIRED. The rep's custody is discharged by the trip-settled update and settlement journal in that same transaction; there is no separate custody balance. prepaid_by_rep sales stay excluded from shift sale-cash attribution.
- INV-GUARD-PRESENCE-PINS (3 November 2026): guard specs pin their invariant entry by presence exactly once, never by last line; exactly one ordering assertion (architecture-guard.spec.ts §9) owns file order — every '- INV-' id unique, newest entry last — and the sale-edit cost-floor guard runs on a seeded open shift, so the Slice 7 P3 gate cannot silently skip its behaviour cases.
- INV-CONVERSION-INTEGRATION-GUARD (3 November 2026): the tax pre-rounding seam and the order/quotation conversion wiring are driven through the real createSale end to end (discounts, payment terms, journal legs read from posted rows); goods conversions deduct no stock through the real path today because convertToSale carries no locationId — a behaviour defect found by this drive, reported, not fixed here — so the conversion cases use service items and the goods-conversion stock leg stays unguarded until a fix package lands.
- INV-VAN-SETTLE-JOURNAL-LEGS (3 November 2026): the settlement journal the real settleTrip posts through the real postVanTripSettlement carries its 1110/1130 legs at tenant book digits on a 3-decimal tenant, with the fils shortage/overage legs to 7200/7100 and a balanced journal; the P6 mock-boundary stub does not guard the journal itself.
- INV-CONVERSION-STOCK-DEDUCTION (3 November 2026): order and quotation conversions carry the branch default stock location into the sale payload (locationId), so a goods conversion deducts stock from the branch's default location exactly like a POS sale — location rows, allocations, movements, and COGS all book at that location. A branch with no default stock location keeps the prior null-location behaviour. createSale's explicit-location validation (active, never damaged/in_transit) still applies to the resolved default and fails closed. The reservation release path is unchanged. Prospective only.
- INV-POS-NET-STOCK-DISPLAY (3 November 2026): the POS catalog display shows the net available quantity checkout enforces — location qty minus reserved_qty over the branch's sellable locations plus the unassigned pool, through the shared availableAcrossLocations helper; the gross sum and the stock_qty minus assigned "discrepancy" add are gone, and a branch-scoped lookup reads the scoped figure even with no explicit locationId (single_location scope is the branch default location). Prospective only.
